      "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n# Build Your Community  All in One Place\n\nFeatsClub connects events, clubs, memberships, fees, fundraising, volunteering, and extracurricular activities—then turns participation into verifiable digital credentials and portfolios that prove social impact for scholarships, grants, and fundraising.\n\n\n[Start as an Organizer](https://my.featsclub.com) [Join as a User](student.html)\n\n![Event 1](./img/s1.png)![Event 2](./img/s2.png)![Event 3](./img/s3.png)![Event 4](./img/s4.png)![Event 5](./img/s5.png)![Event 6](./img/s6.png)![Event 7](./img/s7.png)![Event 8](./img/s8.png)![Event 9](./img/s9.png)![Event 10](./img/s10.png)![Event 11](./img/s11.png)![Event 12](./img/s12.png)\n\n![Event 5](./img/s13.png)![Event 6](./img/s14.png)![Event 7](./img/s15.png)![Event 8](./img/s16.png)![Event 5](./img/s17.png)![Event 6](./img/s18.png)![Event 7](./img/s19.png)![Event 8](./img/s20.png)![Event 8](./img/s21.png)![Event 8](./img/s22.png)![Event 8](./img/s23.png)![Event 8](./img/s24.png)\n\n![Event 9](./img/s25.png)![Event 10](./img/s26.png)![Event 11](./img/s27.png)![Event 12](./img/s28.png)![Event 9](./img/s29.png)![Event 10](./img/30.png)![Event 11](./img/31.png)![Event 12](./img/32.png)![Event 12](./img/33.png)![Event 12](./img/34.png)![Event 12](./img/35.png)![Event 12](./img/36.png)\n\n## Why FeatsClub?\n\n### One Platform. Multiple Solutions. Real  Results.\n\n### Cut costs and operate efficiently. Build your authentic brand and deepen  engagement—all in one place.\n\n![](./img/why1.png)\n\n##### Event Management\n\nPlan events, tickets, classes, RSVPs, and micro-donations easily. Keep participants\nengaged with messaging, volunteers, achievements, and smart AI Q&A.\n\n\n[Learn More](event.html)\n\n![](./img/why7.png)\n\n##### Clubs & Classes\n\nConnect student or member groups with simple attendance, posts, and updates. Handle fees\neffortlessly and keep communities engaged.\n\n\n[Learn More](classess-and-clubs.html)\n\n![](./img/why3.png)\n\n##### Memberships\n\nOffer flexible membership plans with easy renewals and insights. Give members exclusive\naccess, digital badges, and instant AI help.\n\n\n[Learn More](memberships-and-fees.html)\n\n![](./img/why4.png)\n\n##### Donations\n\nRun one-time or recurring campaigns. Engage donors, track impact, and build trust.\nEmpower patrons to launch child campaigns and expand your reach.\n\n\n[Learn More](donation.html)\n\n![](./img/why6.png)\n\n##### FeatsClub Assistant\n\nAll your FeatsClub questions answered. Add chatbots for websites, events, memberships,\nand donation campaigns.\n\n\n[Learn More](coming-soon.html)\n\n![](./img/why9.png)\n\n##### Volunteering\n\nManage volunteer sign-ups and check-in/out, provide student certifications, and grow\nyour\nvolunteer pool with pledges and tracking.\n\n\n[Learn More](community-members-and-users.html)\n\n## Authentic Portfolios for Users & Organizations\n\n### Capture activities, issue verified credentials, and showcase measurable impact—    supporting  scholarships, college readiness, grants, sponsorships, and CSR initiatives.\n\n![](./img/why2.png)\n\n##### Log Activities\n\nLog activities across hundreds of skills with real proof that stays with you for\nlife.\nEmployees record BRG and leadership work to highlight their impact.\n\n\n![](./img/why5.png)\n\n##### Credentials\n\nEndorse everyday achievements and issue digital certificates.\nBuild verifiable portfolios for college, careers, and future opportunities.\n\n![](./img/why8.png)\n\n##### Feats Rewards\n\nEarn digital points & coins for activities, volunteering, and payments, then redeem\nthem\nfor tickets, gifts, or certificates.\n\n\n## Whom We Serve\n\nFeatsClub empowers organizations and individuals to connect, operate, and\nshowcase their impact.\n\n![Whom We Serve](./img/whomweserve.png)\n\n##### Organizations\n\nSchools, Academies, Corporates, and Nonprofits — manage events, memberships, donations,\nand communities in one place.\n\n[See All Solutions](organization.html)\n\n##### Students & Learners\n\nShowcase skills, achievements, and build your portfolio for college or career.\n\n[Learn More](student.html)\n\n##### Community Members\n\nEngage with organizations, volunteer, and highlight your leadership impact.\n\n[Get Started](community-members-and-users.html)\n\n## Join the Purposeful Social Network\n\n### Not Likes. Real Achievements Define Your Net Worth.\n\n![](./img/Memberships1.png)\n\n##### Discover skills, connect with aligned organizations, and showcase  verified achievements on FeatsClub network.\n\n## Become an Ambassador!\n\nJoin today! Embrace a unique opportunity to ignite your passion and drive skill-based community\nclubs!\n\n[Apply Now](our-ambassador.html)\n\n![](./img/ambassador.png)\n\n## Empowering All with AI\n\n### AI that powers every interaction — from buying tickets to running clubs.\n\n🤖\n\n##### Product Assistant\n\nIn-app and web helper for users.\n\n🌐\n\n##### Knowledge Chatbots\n\nEmbeddable bots for organizations on their websites.\n\n🔧\n\n##### Organizer Assistant\n\nFor admins to set up and manage events, memberships, donations, and clubs.\n\n![AI Features](./img/em-screen.png)\n\n![Join FeatsClub](./img/join-cta-logo.png)\n\n### **FeatsClub** voted No. 1 startup by St. Louis   community in  Startup World Cup!\n\n## Engage from Anywhere    with FeatsClub\n\n### Stay connected on desktop or mobile. RSVP, donate, volunteer, or manage your community —  anytime, anywhere.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/ss1.png)\n\n![](./img/ss2.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/ss8.png)\n\n![](./img/ss9.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n###### DOWNLOAD APP ON\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n- [Privacy Policy](privacy-policy.html)\n- [Terms of Use](term-of-use.html)\n- [Terms for Organizations](terms-of-use-for-organizations.html)\n- [Parental Consent](parent-control.html)\n\n- [Funds Organizers](funds-organizers.html)\n- [Funds Donors](funds-donor.html)\n- [Ticket Policy](ticket-policy.html)\n\n- Chesterfield, Missouri, USA 63005\n- [![](./img/facebook.svg)](https://www.facebook.com/featsclub)[![](./img/instagram.svg)](https://www.instagram.com/featsclub)[![](./img/linkedin.svg)](https://www.linkedin.com/company/featsclub)\n\n\nDemo inquiries: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)\n\nPayments, refunds, and other queries:Please contact your organizers.",

      "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n# One Place to Engage, Contribute, and Be Recognized\n\nManage memberships, track volunteer hours, join events, and showcase your contributions—all within a secure and purposeful network designed for meaningful engagement.\n\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/comm.png)\n\n![](img/impact-left.png)\n\n![](img/impact-right.png)\n\n## Impact and Purpose\n\n### 70+ Million\n\npeople volunteer in the U.S. every year\n\n### 2+ Million\n\nnonprofits in the U.S. and 3.7 Million in India need engaged supporters\n\n### Lifelong\n\nImpact through visibility, recognition, and connected purpose\n\n\"Built with the simplicity of social media but the safety of a secure, purpose-driven\nnetwork, FeatsClub ensures your contributions are seen, valued, and remembered for\nlife.\"\n\n### 85%\n\nof volunteers report increased happiness and life satisfaction\n\n### Community\n\nConnect with like-minded individuals and make lasting impact\n\n### Recognition\n\nEarn verified credentials and showcase your community involvement\n\n\"Your time, skills, and passion deserve to be recognized. FeatsClub creates a\npermanent record of your community impact.\"\n\n### Network\n\nBuild meaningful connections with organizations and fellow volunteers\n\n### Growth\n\nDevelop new skills and expand your professional opportunities\n\n### Purpose\n\nMake a real difference in causes that matter to you\n\n\"Join a community where every contribution counts and every volunteer is valued for\ntheir unique impact.\"\n\n### Visibility\n\nShare your volunteer journey with your network and beyond\n\n### Secure\n\nYour data and contributions are protected in a trusted platform\n\n### Forever\n\nYour legacy of service is preserved for future generations\n\n\"FeatsClub combines the ease of modern technology with enterprise-level security to\nprotect your volunteer legacy.\"\n\n## Key Features\n\n##### Lifelong Engagement with Organizations\n\n- Stay connected with nonprofits, schools, and academies\n- Manage memberships, renewals, and history\n- Participate in events, activities, and campaigns\n\n![](./img/communityfeature-1.png)\n\n![](./img/communityfeature-2.png)\n\n##### Recognition & Achievements\n\n- Earn verified badges, certificates, and endorsements\n- Track volunteering hours and skill growth\n- Build a portfolio that supports education and career opportunities\n- Earn Feats Points for volunteering and repeat purchases, redeemable for recognition, rewards, or discounts.\n\n##### Purposeful Network & Community\n\n- Connect with peers who share similar missions and passions\n- Join groups, clubs, and causes that matter to you\n- Engage in discussions, collaborations, and group messaging\n\n![](./img/communityfeature-3.png)\n\n![](./img/communityfeature-4.png)\n\n##### Events & Ticket Management\n\n- RSVP to free events or buy tickets seamlessly\n- Store and access your passes digitally\n- Quick check-ins and confirmations\n\n##### Memberships & Donations\n\n- Manage fees, memberships, and renewals\n- Contribute donations securely in one place\n- See how your contributions are making a difference in community impact\n- Receive Feats Points for continued support, which organizations can use to offer special perks or discounts\n\n![](./img/communityfeature-5.png)\n\n![](./img/communityfeature-6.png)\n\n##### Long-term Connection\n\n- Stay recognized even after graduation or transitions\n- Remain part of alumni groups, patrons, and community networks\n- Continue your journey of contribution and recognition for life\n\n## How It Works\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/communitystep-1.png)\n\n##### 1 Get Invited\n\nOrganizations invite you to join\ntheir events, memberships,\ndonations, or classes/groups.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/communitystep-2.png)\n\n##### 2 Join & Engage\n\nDownload the FeatsClub app\n(or use web) to register, RSVP,\ncontribute, and stay engaged.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/communitystep-3.png)\n\n##### 3 Track & Build Your  Impact\n\nKeep all records—donations,\nmemberships, volunteering\nhours, and achievements—\nsecure in one place for\nauthentic recognition.\n\n## Built for Modern Communities\n\nEnterprise-grade security meets user-friendly design with features built for today's\ndigital-first world.\n\n##### Mobile-First Design\n\nOptimized for social media-friendly users\nwith easy, modern, yet secure interfaces\nthat drive engagement.\n\n##### Complete Web Access\n\nWeb interfaces for events, tickets,\ndonations, memberships, and\ncomprehensive administration.\n\n##### Trusted Cloud Platform\n\nHosted on Google Firebase, compliant\nwith GDPR, CCPA, SOC 1/2/3—with parent\nconsent required for children under 13.\n\n##### Secure Data Architecture\n\nMulti-tenant setup with logical or isolated\ndatabases, ensuring your data stays\nsecure and compliant.\n\n##### Streamlined Payments\n\nStripe integration for tickets, donations,\nand membership payments—secure,\nreliable, and transparent\n\n##### Advanced Analytics\n\nGoal tracking, activity reports, and AI-powered insights for better community\nengagement and growth.\n\n![](./img/gg13.png)\n\n## Every student deserves to be recognized.\n\n## Every skill deserves to be remembered.\n\nFeatsClub helps you grow, connect, and prepare for what’s next.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n######       "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n# Start Your Classes & Clubs. Manage Everything in One Place.\n\nFrom academic clubs to extracurricular classes — FeatsClub\nhelps you set up groups, manage rosters, track progress, and\nbuild recognition for every student or member.\n\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/clubhero.png)\n\n![](./img/pattern.png)\n\n## Clubs & Classes That Empower Students and Communities\n\n\"FeatsClub has helped us organize 100+ clubs with ease. What\nused to be manual and scattered is now transparent, engaging, and rewarding for\nstudents. Parents and teachers finally have one place to see participation and\nachievements.\"\n\nAnanya Sharma, School Administrator\n\nJain University\n\n## Clubs & Classes That Empower Students and Communities\n\n\"Managing multiple classes and extracurricular activities\nhas never been easier. FeatsClub streamlines everything from enrollment to progress\ntracking, giving us more time to focus on what matters most - our students.\"\n\nMichael Chen, Program Director\n\nCommunity Learning Center\n\n## Clubs & Classes That Empower Students and Communities\n\n\"The platform has transformed how we run our youth clubs.\nFrom attendance tracking to achievement recognition, every feature is thoughtfully\ndesigned. Our engagement rates have increased significantly.\"\n\nSarah Williams, Club Coordinator\n\nYouth Development Organization\n\n## Clubs & Classes That Empower Students and Communities\n\n\"FeatsClub provides a comprehensive solution for managing\nall our educational programs. The ability to track student progress and maintain\ndetailed records has been invaluable for our reporting and accreditation needs.\"\n\nDr. Rajesh Kumar, Academic Dean\n\nInternational School\n\n## Not Just Management. A Growth Platform.\n\nFeatsClub gives organizers modern tools to go beyond attendance and scheduling — helping you build\nthriving communities\nwhere every participant's growth and achievements are recognized.\n\n![Platform Features](./img/ff1.png)\n\n#### Streamlined Setup\n\nCreate and manage clubs or classes in minutes with built-in rosters, announcements,\nand scheduling.\n\n#### Engage & Motivate\n\nTrack attendance, issue credentials, and celebrate milestones with digital\nrecognition.\n\n#### Build Lasting Communities\n\nKeep parents, alumni, and patrons connected through announcements, achievements, and\nupdates.\n\n## Everything You Need to Run Classes & Clubs — All in One Platform\n\n##### Club & Class Setup\n\nEasily create clubs, groups, or classes with configurable structures.\n\n##### Schedules & Attendance\n\nPost schedules, track attendance, and delegate admin roles.\n\n##### Announcements & Messaging\n\nShare updates, news, and posts with students, parents, or members.\n\n##### Credentials & Recognition\n\nIssue digital certificates for participation, achievements, or skill levels.\n\n##### Assignments & Practice Logs\n\nTrack student assignments, practice hours, and skill progression.\n\n##### Parent & Patron Engagement\n\nKeep parents informed and patrons connected with progress and milestones.\n\n##### Integrated with Events & Fees\n\nLink clubs/classes with event registrations, RSVPs, and membership/fee payments.\n\n\n##### AI Support\n\nAI assistant for FAQs, scheduling support, and personalized activity insights.\n\n\n![](./img/gg12.png)\n\n## Simple to Launch. Built to Grow.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/classestep-1.png)\n\n##### 1 Create Your Club/Class\n\nSet up groups with rosters,\nschedules, and goals.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/classestep-2.png)\n\n##### 2 Invite & Engage Members\n\nShare sign-up links, invite\nparents/students, and start\ntracking participation.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/classestep-3.png)\n\n##### 3 Recognize & Grow\n\nIssue certificates, track skills,\nand build authentic student/\nmember portfolios.\n\n## Every student deserves to be recognized.\n\n## Every skill deserves to be remembered.\n\nFeatsClub helps you grow, connect, and prepare for what’s next.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n###### DOWNLOAD APP ON\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n- [Privacy Policy](privacy-policy.html)\n- [Terms of Use](term-of-use.html)\n- [Terms for Organizations](terms-of-use-for-organizations.html)\n- [Parental Consent](parent-control.html)\n\n- [Funds Organizers](funds-organizers.html)\n- [Funds Donors](funds-donor.html)\n- [Ticket Policy](ticket-policy.html)\n\n- Chesterfield, Missouri, USA 63005\n- [![](./img/facebook.svg)](https://www.facebook.com/featsclub)[![](./img/instagram.svg)](https://www.instagram.com/featsclub)[![](./img/linkedin.svg)](https://www.linkedin.com/company/featsclub)\n\n\nDemo inquiries: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)\n\nPayments, refunds, and other queries:Please contact your organizers.",

      "markdown": "Last Modified: Jan 29, 2025\n\n## Terms and Conditions for Organizations\n\nThese Terms and Conditions and the executed Order Form or Subscriptions (the “Order”)\nconstitute Customer’s agreement with FeatsClub Inc. (“Provider”) (the “Agreement”). Provider\nand Customer may be referred to herein collectively as the \" **Parties**\" or individually\nas a \" **Party**.\"\n\n\n\nNOW, THEREFORE, in consideration of the mutual covenants, terms, and conditions set forth\nherein, and for other good and valuable consideration, the receipt and sufficiency of which\nare hereby acknowledged, the Parties agree as follows:\n\n\n## Definitions\n\n- **\"Aggregated Statistics\"** means data and information related to Customer's use of\n   the Services that is used by Provider in an aggregate and anonymized manner, including\n   to compile statistical and performance information related to the provision and\n   operation of the Services.\n\n- **\"Authorized User\"** means Customer's employees, consultants, contractors, and\n   agents (i) who are authorized by Customer to access and use the Services under the\n   rights granted to Customer pursuant to this Agreement and (ii) for whom access to the\n   Services has been purchased hereunder.\n\n- **\"Customer Data\"** means, other than Aggregated Statistics, information, data, and\n   other content, in any form or medium, that is submitted, posted, or otherwise\n   transmitted by or on behalf of Customer or an Authorized User through the Services.\n\n- **\"Documentation\"** means end-user documentation relating to the Services available\n   at [https://www.featsclub.com](https://www.featsclub.com)\n- **\"Provider IP\"** means the Services, the Documentation, and any and all intellectual\n   property provided to Customer or any Authorized User in connection with the foregoing.\n   For the avoidance of doubt, Provider IP includes Aggregated Statistics and any\n   information, data, or other content derived from Provider's monitoring of Customer's\n   access to or use of the Services, but does not include Customer Data.\n\n- **\"Services\"** means the software-as-a-service offering described in the Order.\n\n\n## Access and Use.\n\n- **Provision of Access :** Subject to and conditioned on Customer's payment of Fees\n   and compliance with all other terms and conditions of this Agreement, Provider hereby\n   grants Customer a non-exclusive, non-transferable right to access and use the Services\n   during the Term, solely for use by Authorized Users in accordance with the terms and\n   conditions herein. Such use is limited to Customer's internal use. Provider shall\n   provide to Customer the necessary passwords and network links or connections to allow\n   Customer to access the Services. The total number of Authorized Users will not exceed\n   the number set forth in the Order, except as expressly agreed to in writing by the\n   Parties and subject to any appropriate adjustment of the Fees payable hereunder.\n\n- **Documentation License :** Subject to the terms and conditions contained in this\n   Agreement, Provider hereby grants to Customer a non-exclusive, non-sublicensable,\n   non-transferable license to use the Documentation during the Term solely for Customer's\n   internal business purposes in connection with its use of the Services.\n\n- **Use Restrictions :** Customer shall not use the Services for any purposes beyond\n   the scope of the access granted in this Agreement. Customer shall not at any time,\n   directly or indirectly, and shall not permit any Authorized Users to: (i) copy, modify,\n   or create derivative works of the Services or Documentation, in whole or in part; (ii)\n   rent, lease, lend, sell, license, sublicense, assign, distribute, publish, transfer, or\n   otherwise make available the Services or Documentation; (iii) reverse engineer,\n   disassemble, decompile, decode, adapt, or otherwise attempt to derive or gain access to\n   any software component of the Services, in whole or in part; (iv) remove any proprietary\n   notices from the Services or Documentation; or (v) use the Services or Documentation in\n   any manner or for any purpose that infringes, misappropriates, or otherwise violates any\n   intellectual property right or other right of any person, or that violates any\n   applicable law.\n\n- **Reservation of Rights :** Provider reserves all rights not expressly granted to\n   Customer in this Agreement. Except for the limited rights and licenses expressly granted\n   under this Agreement, nothing in this Agreement grants, by implication, waiver,\n   estoppel, or otherwise, to Customer or any third party any intellectual property rights\n   or other right, title, or interest in or to the Provider IP.\n\n- **Suspension :** Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this Agreement,\n   Provider may temporarily suspend Customer's and any Authorized User's access to any\n   portion or all of the Services if: (i) Provider reasonably determines that (A) there is\n   a threat or attack on any of the Provider IP; (B) Customer's or any Authorized User's\n   use of the Provider IP disrupts or poses a security risk to the Provider IP or to any\n   other customer or vendor of Provider; (C) Customer, or any Authorized User, is using the\n   Provider IP for fraudulent or illegal activities; (D) subject to applicable law,\n   Customer has ceased to continue its business in the ordinary course, made an assignment\n   for the benefit of creditors or similar disposition of its assets, or become the subject\n   of any bankruptcy, reorganization, liquidation, dissolution, or similar proceeding; or\n   (E) Provider's provision of the Services to Customer or any Authorized User is\n   prohibited by applicable law; (ii) any vendor of Provider has suspended or terminated\n   Provider's access to or use of any third-party services or products required to enable\n   Customer to access the Services; or (iii) in accordance with Section 4(a)(iii) (any such\n   suspension described in subclause (i), (ii), or (iii), a “ **Service Suspension**”).\n   Provider shall use commercially reasonable efforts to provide written notice of any\n   Service Suspension to Customer and to provide updates regarding resumption of access to\n   the Services following any Service Suspension. Provider shall use commercially\n   reasonable efforts to resume providing access to the Services as soon as reasonably\n   possible after the event giving rise to the Service Suspension is cured. Provider will\n   have no liability for any damage, liabilities, losses (including any loss of data or\n   profits), or any other consequences that Customer or any Authorized User may incur as a\n   result of a Service Suspension.\n\n- **Aggregated Statistics :** Notwithstanding anything to the contrary in this\n   Agreement, Provider may monitor Customer's use of the Services and collect and compile\n   Aggregated Statistics. As between Provider and Customer, all right, title, and interest\n   in Aggregated Statistics, and all intellectual property rights therein, belong to and\n   are retained solely by Provider. Customer acknowledges that Provider may compile\n   Aggregated Statistics based on Customer Data input into the Services. Customer agrees\n   that Provider may (i) make Aggregated Statistics publicly available in compliance with\n   applicable law, and (ii) use Aggregated Statistics to the extent and in the manner\n   permitted under applicable law; provided that such Aggregated Statistics do not identify\n   Customer or Customer's Confidential Information.\n\n\n## Customer Responsibilities.\n\n- **General :** Customer is responsible and liable for all uses of the Services and\n   Documentation resulting from access provided by Customer, directly or indirectly,\n   whether such access or use is permitted by or in violation of this Agreement. Without\n   limiting the generality of the foregoing, Customer is responsible for all acts and\n   omissions of Authorized Users, and any act or omission by an Authorized User that would\n   constitute a breach of this Agreement if taken by Customer will be deemed a breach of\n   this Agreement by Customer. Customer shall use reasonable efforts to make all Authorized\n   Users aware of this Agreement's provisions as applicable to such Authorized User's use\n   of the Services, and shall cause Authorized Users to comply with such provisions.\n\n- **Customer Data :** The Parties understand and agree that use of the Services\n   involves the receipt and processing by Provider of personally identifiable information\n   of Customer’s Authorized Users (“Customer Data”). As between the Parties, Customer Data\n   is, and remains, the property of Customer as controller of the Customer Data, and\n   Provider acts as service provider and processor of the Customer Data under this\n   Agreement. Provider confirms that it will use Customer Data solely to enable Provider to\n   provision and support its Services and operations, to fulfill its obligations to\n   Customer under and in accordance with this Agreement, and as provided under applicable\n   law.\n\n\n\n\n   Customer warrants to Provider: (i) that it has all rights, licenses, permissions, and\n   authorities necessary to enter into this Agreement; and (ii) that its provision of\n   Customer Data (as defined herein) to Provider and its authorizations and instructions to\n   Provider relating to the processing of such Customer Data shall at all times be in\n   compliance with all applicable laws and regulations, including data protection laws and\n   any notice and/or consent requirements.\n\n\n\n## Fees and Payment\n\n- **Fees :** Customer shall pay Provider the fees (\" **Fees**\") as set forth in the\n   Order without offset or deduction. Customer shall make all payments hereunder in US\n   dollars on or before the due date set forth in the Order. If Customer fails to make any\n   payment when due, without limiting Provider's other rights and remedies: (i) Provider\n   may charge interest on the past due amount at the rate of 1.5% per month calculated\n   daily and compounded monthly or, if lower, the highest rate permitted under applicable\n   law; (ii) Customer shall reimburse Provider for all costs incurred by Provider in\n   collecting any late payments or interest, including reasonable attorneys' fees, court\n   costs, and collection agency fees; and (iii) if such failure continues for 10 days or\n   more, Provider may suspend Customer's and its Authorized Users' access to any portion or\n   all of the Services until such amounts are paid in full.\n\n\n- **Taxes :** All Fees and other amounts payable by Customer under this Agreement are\n   exclusive of taxes and similar assessments. Customer is responsible for all sales, use,\n   and excise taxes, and any other similar taxes, duties, and charges of any kind imposed\n   by any federal, state, or local governmental or regulatory authority on any amounts\n   payable by Customer hereunder, other than any taxes imposed on Provider's income.\n\n- **Auditing Rights and Required Records :** Customer agrees to maintain complete and\n   accurate records in accordance with generally accepted accounting principles during the\n   Term and for a period of two years after the termination or expiration of this Agreement\n   with respect to matters necessary for accurately determining amounts due hereunder.\n   Provider may, at its own expense, on reasonable prior notice, periodically inspect and\n   audit Customer's records with respect to matters covered by this Agreement, provided\n   that if such inspection and audit reveals that Customer has underpaid Provider with\n   respect to any amounts due and payable during the Term, Customer shall promptly pay the\n   amounts necessary to rectify such underpayment, together with interest in accordance\n   with Section 4(a). Customer shall pay for the costs of the audit if the audit determines\n   that Customer's underpayment equals or exceeds 5% for any quarter. Such inspection and\n   auditing rights will extend throughout the Term of this Agreement and for a period of\n   Two years after the termination or expiration of this Agreement.\n\n\n## Confidential Information\n\nFrom time to time during the Term, either Party may disclose or make available to the other\nParty information about its business affairs, products, confidential intellectual property,\ntrade secrets, third-party confidential information, and other sensitive or proprietary\ninformation, whether orally or in written, electronic, or other form or media/in written or\nelectronic form or media, and whether or not marked, designated, or otherwise identified as\n\"confidential\" (collectively, \" **Confidential Information**\"). Confidential Information\ndoes not include information that, at the time of disclosure is: (a) in the public domain;\n(b) known to the receiving Party at the time of disclosure; (c) rightfully obtained by the\nreceiving Party on a non-confidential basis from a third party; or (d) independently\ndeveloped by the receiving Party. The receiving Party shall not disclose the disclosing\nParty's Confidential Information to any person or entity, except to the receiving Party's\nemployees who have a need to know the Confidential Information for the receiving Party to\nexercise its rights or perform its obligations hereunder. Notwithstanding the foregoing,\neach Party may disclose Confidential Information to the limited extent required (i) in order\nto comply with the order of a court or other governmental body, or as otherwise necessary to\ncomply with applicable law, provided that the Party making the disclosure pursuant to the\norder shall first have given written notice to the other Party and made a reasonable effort\nto obtain a protective order; or (ii) to establish a Party's rights under this Agreement,\nincluding to make required court filings. On the expiration or termination of the Agreement,\nthe receiving Party shall promptly return to the disclosing Party all copies, whether in\nwritten, electronic, or other form or media, of the disclosing Party's Confidential\nInformation, or destroy all such copies and certify in writing to the disclosing Party that\nsuch Confidential Information has been destroyed. Each Party's obligations of non-disclosure\nwith regard to Confidential Information are effective as of the Effective Date and will\nexpire five years from the date first disclosed to the receiving Party; provided, however,\nwith respect to any Confidential Information that constitutes a trade secret (as determined\nunder applicable law), such obligations of non-disclosure will survive the termination or\nexpiration of this Agreement for as long as such Confidential Information remains subject to\ntrade secret protection under applicable law.\n\n## Intellectual Property Ownership; Feedback.\n\n- **Provider IP :** Customer acknowledges that, as between Customer and Provider,\n   Provider owns all right, title, and interest, including all intellectual property\n   rights, in and to the Provider IP and, with respect to third-party products, the\n   applicable third-party providers own all right, title, and interest, including all\n   intellectual property rights, in and to the third-party products.\n\n- **Customer Data :** Provider acknowledges that, as between Provider and Customer,\n   Customer owns all right, title, and interest, including all intellectual property\n   rights, in and to the Customer Data. PROVIDER SPECIFICALLY\nDISCLAIMS ALL IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE,\nTITLE, AND NON-INFRINGEMENT, AND ALL WARRANTIES ARISING FROM COURSE OF DEALING, USAGE, OR\nTRADE PRACTICE. PROVIDER MAKES NO WARRANTY OF ANY KIND THAT THE PROVIDER IP, OR ANY PRODUCTS\nOR RESULTS OF THE USE THEREOF, WILL MEET CUSTOMER'S OR ANY OTHER PERSON'S REQUIREMENTS,\nOPERATE WITHOUT INTERRUPTION, ACHIEVE ANY INTENDED RESULT, BE COMPATIBLE OR WORK WITH ANY\nSOFTWARE, SYSTEM, OR OTHER SERVICES, OR BE SECURE, ACCURATE, COMPLETE, FREE OF HARMFUL CODE,\nOR ERROR FREE.\n\n## Indemnification.\n\n- **Provider Indemnification :**\n\n\n   Provider shall indemnify, defend, and hold harmless Customer from and against any and\n   all losses, damages, liabilities, costs (including reasonable attorneys' fees)\n   (\" **Losses**\") incurred by Customer resulting from any third-party claim, suit,\n   action, or proceeding (\" **Third-Party Claim**\") that the Services, or any use of the\n   Services in accordance with this Agreement, infringes or misappropriates such third\n   party's US patents, copyrights, or trade secrets, provided that Customer promptly\n   notifies Provider in writing of such Third-Party Claim, cooperates with Provider, and\n   allows Provider sole authority to control the defense and settlement of such Third-Party\n   Claim.\n\n\n\n   If a Third Party-Claim is made or appears possible, Provider may, at its sole\n   discretion, to (A) modify or replace the Services, or component or part thereof, to make\n   it non-infringing, or (B) obtain the right for Customer to continue use. or (v) modifications to\n   the Services not made by Provider, provided that Customer may not settle any Third-Party\n   Claim against Provider unless Provider consents to such settlement, and further provided\n   that Provider will have the right, at its option, to defend itself against any such\n   Third-Party Claim or to participate in the defense thereof by counsel of its own choice.\n\n- **Sole Remedy :** THIS 9 SETS FORTH CUSTOMER'S SOLE REMEDIES AND PROVIDER'S SOLE\n   LIABILITY AND OBLIGATION FOR ANY ACTUAL, THREATENED, OR ALLEGED CLAIMS THAT THE SERVICES\n   INFRINGE, MISAPPROPRIATE, OR OTHERWISE VIOLATE ANY INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY RIGHTS OF ANY\n   THIRD PARTY.\n\n\n## Limitations of Liability.\n\nIN NO EVENT WILL PROVIDER BE LIABLE UNDER OR IN CONNECTION WITH THIS AGREEMENT UNDER ANY\nLEGAL OR EQUITABLE THEORY, INCLUDING BREACH OF CONTRACT, TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE), STRICT\nLIABILITY, AND OTHERWISE, FOR ANY: (a) CONSEQUENTIAL, INCIDENTAL, INDIRECT, EXEMPLARY,\nSPECIAL, ENHANCED, OR PUNITIVE DAMAGES; OR (e) COST OF REPLACEMENT GOODS OR SERVICES, IN EACH CASE REGARDLESS OF\nWHETHER PROVIDER WAS ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH LOSSES OR DAMAGES OR SUCH LOSSES OR\nDAMAGES WERE OTHERWISE FORESEEABLE. IN NO EVENT WILL PROVIDER'S AGGREGATE LIABILITY ARISING\nOUT OF OR RELATED TO THIS AGREEMENT UNDER ANY LEGAL OR EQUITABLE THEORY, INCLUDING BREACH OF\nCONTRACT, TORT (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE), STRICT LIABILITY, AND OTHERWISE EXCEED THE TOTAL\nAMOUNTS PAID TO PROVIDER UNDER THIS AGREEMENT IN THE 12-MONTH PERIOD PRECEDING THE EVENT\nGIVING RISE TO THE CLAIM.\n\n\n## Term and Termination.\n\n- **Termination :** In addition to any other express termination right set forth in\n   this Agreement:\n\n\n\n   Provider may terminate this Agreement, effective on written notice to Customer, if\n   Customer: (A) fails to pay any amount when due hereunder, and such failure continues\n   more than 10 days after Provider's delivery of written notice thereof; or (B) breaches\n   any of its obligations under Section 2(c) or 5;\n\n\n\n   either Party may terminate this Agreement, effective on written notice to the other\n   Party, if the other Party materially breaches this Agreement, and such breach: (A) is\n   incapable of cure; or (B) being capable of cure, remains uncured 30 days after the\n   non-breaching Party provides the breaching Party with written notice of such breach; or\n\n\n\n   either Party may terminate this Agreement, effective immediately upon written notice to\n   the other Party, if the other Party: (A) becomes insolvent or is generally unable to\n   pay, or fails to pay, its debts as they become due; (B) files or has filed against it, a\n   petition for voluntary or involuntary bankruptcy or otherwise becomes subject,\n   voluntarily or involuntarily, to any proceeding under any domestic or foreign bankruptcy\n   or insolvency law; (C) makes or seeks to make a general assignment for the benefit of\n   its creditors; or (D) applies for or has appointed a receiver, trustee, custodian, or\n   similar agent appointed by order of any court of competent jurisdiction to take charge\n   of or sell any material portion of its property or business.\n\n\n\n\n\n   either Party may terminate this Agreement, effective immediately upon\n   written notice to the other Party, if the other Party: (A) becomes insolvent or is\n   generally\n   unable to pay, or fails to pay, its debts as they become due; (B) files or has filed\n   against\n   it, a petition for voluntary or involuntary bankruptcy or otherwise becomes subject,\n   voluntarily or involuntarily, to any proceeding under any domestic or foreign bankruptcy\n   or insolvency law; (C) makes or seeks to make a general assignment for the benefit of\n   its\n   creditors; or (D) applies for or has appointed a receiver, trustee, custodian, or\n   similar\n   agent appointed by order of any court of competent jurisdiction to take charge of or\n   sell\n   any material portion of its property or business.\n\n\n- **Effect of Expiration or Termination :** Upon expiration or earlier termination of\n   this Agreement, Customer shall immediately discontinue use of the Provider IP and,\n   without limiting Customer's obligations under 5, Customer shall delete, destroy, or\n   return all copies of the Provider IP and certify in writing to the Provider that the\n   Provider IP has been deleted or destroyed. No expiration or termination will affect\n   Customer's obligation to pay all Fees that may have become due before such expiration or\n   termination or entitle Customer to any refund.\n\n- **Survival :** This Section 11(d) and 1, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8(b), 9, 10, and 12 survive any\n   termination or expiration of this Agreement. No other provisions of this Agreement\n   survive the expiration or earlier termination of this Agreement.\n\n\n## Miscellaneous.\n\n- **Entire Agreement :** This Agreement and an Order constitute the entire agreement\n   between the parties and supersede all previous agreements, written or oral, between the\n   parties with respect to the subject matter hereof. Any preprinted terms and conditions on a purchase\n   order, quotation, acknowledgement, invoice, or similar document that conflict with the\n   terms of this Agreement are deemed superseded by this Agreement.\n\n- **Notices :** All notices, requests, consents, claims, demands, waivers, and other\n   communications hereunder (each, a \"Notice\") must be in writing and addressed to the\n   Parties at the addresses set forth on the first page of this Agreement. All notices must\n   be in writing and sent by United States certified mail, postage prepaid, return receipt\n   requested (“US Mail”) or postage prepaid United States Express Mail (“Express Mail”),\n   and will be deemed given (a) the next business day after posting, if sent by US Mail,\n   and (b) the next business day, if sent by Courier.\n\n- **Force Majeure :** In no event shall either Party be liable to the other Party, or\n   be deemed to have breached this Agreement, for any failure or delay in performing its\n   obligations under this Agreement (except for any obligations to make payments), if and\n   to the extent such failure or delay is caused by any circumstances beyond such Party's\n   reasonable control, including but not limited to acts of God, flood, fire, earthquake,\n   epidemics, pandemics, explosion, war, terrorism, cyberattacks, invasion, riot or other\n   civil unrest, or passage of law or any action taken by a governmental or public\n   authority.\n\n- **Amendment and Modification; Waiver :** No amendment to or modification of this\n   Agreement is effective unless it is in writing and signed by an authorized\n   representative of each Party. No course of dealing, course of performance, or failure of\n   either party to strictly enforce any provision in the body of this Agreement is to be\n   construed as a waiver thereof.\n\n- **Severability :** If any provision of this Agreement is held illegal, invalid or\n   unenforceable under the Law, such provision will be fully severable and this Agreement\n   will be construed and enforced as if such illegal, invalid or unenforceable provision\n   were not a part of this Agreement.\n\n- **Governing Law; Submission to Jurisdiction :** This Agreement is governed by and\n   construed in accordance with the internal laws of the State of Missouri without giving\n   effect to any choice or conflict of law provision or rule that would require or permit\n   the application of the laws of any jurisdiction other than those of the State of\n   Missouri. caressing).\n\n\n  Child grooming (for example, befriending a child online to facilitate, either online\n   or offline, sexual contact and/or exchanging sexual imagery with that child).\n\n  Sexualization of a minor (for example, imagery that depicts, encourages or promotes\n   the sexual abuse of children or the portrayal of children in a manner that could\n   result in the sexual person's physical appearance.\n\n\n   We will take appropriate action, including but not limited to removing such content and\n   reporting it to the National Center for Missing & Exploited Children if we become\n   aware of content containing child sexual abuse materials.\n\n\n- **Equitable Relief :** Each Party acknowledges and agrees that a breach or\n   threatened breach by such Party of any       "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n# Build Your Skills. Get Recognized. Shape Your Future.\n\nFeatsClub helps students log skills, track achievements,\nparticipate in clubs and academies, earn recognition, and build\nauthentic records that prepare you for college, careers, and life.\n\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/student.png)\n\n## Impact and Purpose\n\n![](./img/purpose.png)\n\n##### 80%+ of U.S. high school students participate in  extracurricular activities (sports, arts, clubs, volunteering)\n\n##### 90% of employers value extracurricular involvement when  evaluating candidates\n\n##### 75% of students report improved time management skills through  extracurricular participation\n\n##### 85% of college admissions officers consider extracurricular  activities important in their decisions\n\n###### \"Built with the simplicity of social media but the safety of a secure, purpose-driven  network, FeatsClub ensures your contributions are seen, valued, and remembered for life.\n\n## Key Benefits\n\n![](./img/student-benefit-1.png)\n\n##### Skills & Everyday Achievements\n\n- Log your skills, practice hours, and daily activities\n- Earn endorsements from teachers, coaches, and mentors\n- Celebrate milestones with digital certificates and rewards\n\n##### Clubs & Academies Participation\n\n- Join school clubs and get recognized for your contributions\n- Engage deeply in extracurricular academies—track levels, promotions, and progress\n- Participate in events and competitions with seamless check-ins and recognition\n\n![](./img/student-benefit-2.png)\n\n![](./img/student-benefit-3.png)\n\n##### Volunteering & Community Service\n\n- Track hours of volunteering for authentic service records\n- Build leadership and community engagement credentials\n- Generate verified reports to use in college applications\n\n##### Recognition & Rewards\n\n- Earn Feats Points for contributions like volunteering, club activities, or academy participation\n- Redeem Feats Points for recognition, perks, or discounts offered by organizations\n- Gain visibility for achievements that might otherwise go unnoticed\n\n![](./img/student-benefit-4.png)\n\n![](./img/student-benefit-5.png)\n\n##### Future-Ready Portfolio\n\n- Consolidate achievements, certificates, and reports in one place\n- Share your authentic student transcript with colleges or scholarship programs\n- Highlight skills and leadership growth for career opportunities\n\n##### Purposeful Student Network\n\n- Connect with peers practicing similar skills—arts, sports, STEM, service, and more\n- Join discussions, collaborations, and group activities\n- Learn from and inspire others in a safe, secure environment\n\n![](./img/student-benefit-6.png)\n\n## How It Works\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/studentstep-1.png)\n\n##### 1 Join Your School or  Academy\n\nGet invited by your school,\nclub, or academy or sign up\nto track your own activities\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/studentstep-2.png)\n\n##### 2 Log Your Skills &  Activities\n\nRecord achievements,\nservice hours, certificates,\nand milestones from\nanywhere.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/studentstep-3.png)\n\n##### 3 Build Recognition &  Future Opportunities\n\nEarn endorsements, points,\nand authentic reports you can\nuse for college and beyond.\n\n## Built for Students, Backed by Enterprise Security\n\nSafe, modern, and student-friendly designed with simplicity, recognition, and your future in mind.\n\n\n##### Mobile-First Design\n\nSimple, social-friendly, secure interfaces for everyday logging.\n\n##### Complete Web Access\n\nManage events, clubs, and achievements online anytime.\n\n##### Trusted Cloud Platform\n\nHosted on Google Firebase, GDPR/CCPA compliant, with parental consent for students under\n13.\n\n##### Secure Data Aour-ambassador.html    Multi-tenant isolation to ensure student records remain private and safe.\n\n##### Streamlined Engagement\n\nTrack club participation, academy involvement, service hours, and events seamlessly.\n\n##### AI Advisory (Coming Soon)\n\nPersonalized AI guidance for skills enrichment, leadership growth, and college readiness.\n\n\n## Every student deserves to be recognized.\n\n## Every skill deserves to be remembered.\n\nFeatsClub helps you grow, connect, and prepare for what’s next.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n######       "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n# Operating System for Non-Profit Communities\n\nA complete community platform that unifies memberships, events,\nfundraising, volunteer tracking, and engagement tools—all in one modern, intuitive system.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/non-profit-hero.png)\n\n## Complete Solutions for Non Profits\n\nEverything your organization needs to engage communities for life, track\nimpact, and\n\ngrow sustainably—all integrated into one powerful platform.\n\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn1.png)\n\n#### Smart Membership Management\n\n- Student, individual, family, or lifetime membership options\n- Capture complete participant details including family members\n- Unique Member IDs with secure record-keeping for compliance\n- Automated member communications and renewal reminders\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn2.png)\n\n#### Non-profits & Community Organizations\n\n- Free events with micro-donations and RSVP forms\n- Paid ticketing with guest fees, coupons, and email confirmations\n- Group Registrations for Competitions.\n- QR code check-ins for efficient attendance tracking\n- Real-time event updates via posts and mobile notifications\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn3.png)\n\n#### Comprehensive Fundraising Platform\n\n- Direct donations and patron-led campaign management\n- Micro-donations integrated with free event RSVPs\n- Automated impact updates that keep donors engaged\n- Professional tax receipt generation and donor recognition\n- Long-term relationship building for sustained contributions\n\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn4.png)\n\n#### Volunteer & Recognition System\n\n- Comprehensive volunteer enrollment and hour tracking\n- Digital certificates and endorsements for recognition\n\n- Club and vocational program management for leadership building\n\n- Activity configurations for diverse community service types\n\n- Verifiable reports for grants, compliance, and impact measurement\n\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn5.png)\n\n#### Club & Group Management\n\n- Set up classes and groups easily\n- Manage schedules and track attendance\n\n- Enable group messaging and communication\n\n- Issue credentials for participation\n\n- Delegate management responsibilities\n\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn6.png)\n\n#### Communication & Administration\n\n- Assign delegates to manage organization modules\n- Track member start/join dates automatically\n- Post announcements to all members instantly\n\n- Mobile push notifications for important updates\n\n- Centralized communication dashboard\n\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn7.png)\n\n#### Skills Management\n\n- Configure diverse activities (food drives, kids programs, language learning)\n- Endorse users for activities and service hours\n\n- Issue digital certificates for key accomplishments\n\n\n- Prepare verifiable reports for organizations and users\n\n- Track long-term engagement and impact\n\n\n![Schools & Universities](./img/cmn8.png)\n\n#### AI ChatBots\n\n- Ingest knowledge about your organization automatically\n- Deploy chatbots on your websites and platforms\n\n- Enhance user experience with instant chat support\n\n- Answer common questions about events and programs\n\n- Reduce administrative workload with automated responses\n\n\n## Save Costs with Integrated Tools\n\nStop paying multiple vendors with hidden fees. FeatsClub's transparent, all-in-one\nsolution\n\nreduces costs while increasing engagement and efficiency.\n\n##### Avoid High Commissions\n\nTransparent pricing with no hidden platform fees or surprise charges that\neat into your budget\n\n##### No Excessive Tips for Payments\n\nDirect payment processing without forcing additional tips or convenience\ncharges on your supporters\n\n##### Own Your Community in One Place\n\nEnhanced engagement through unified platform ownership rather than\nscattered tools and vendors\n\n## Our Success Story\n\n### ![](./img/nonprofit-organization.png)\n\n##### Nonprofit Cultural Organization\n\n\"As a nonprofit cultural organization, we moved to FeatsClub for all our operations and membership engagement. It not only supported our 40th-year celebration with multi-day events, but also helped us digitize every aspect of member engagement for record keeping, event management, and guest ticketing.\"\n\n\n_(Full testimonial available upon request)_\n\n![](./img/succ1.png)\n\n## Built for Modern Communities\n\nEnterprise-grade security meets user-friendly design with features\ndesigned for today's digital-first world.\n\n\n##### Mobile-First Design\n\nOptimized for social-media-friendly users with easy, modern yet\nsecure interfaces that drive engagement.\n\n\n##### Complete Web Access\n\nWeb interfaces\n\nFor events, tickets, donations, memberships, and comprehensive\nadministration.\n\n\n##### Trusted Cloud Platform\n\nHosted on Google Firebase, compliant with GDPR, CCPA, SOC 1/2/3 with\nparent consent for children under 13.\n\n\n##### Secure Data Architecture\n\nMulti-tenant setup with logical or isolated databases ensuring your\ndata stays secure and compliant.\n\n\n##### Streamlined Payments\n\nStripe platform integration for all tickets, donations, and\nmembership payment processing with transparent pricing.\n\n\n##### Advanced Analytics\n\nDec 2025\n\nGoal tracking, activity reports, and AI-powered insights for better\ncommunity engagement and growth.\n\n\n## Get Started in Minutes, Not Months\n\n![](./img/shape-bg.png)![](img/nonprofitstep-1.png)\n\n##### 1 Download & Setup\n\nInstall the FeatsClub app and set up your organization account with a\ncommon administrator email. Verify your organization and connect your bank account via\nStripe.\n\n![](./img/shape-bg.png)![](img/nonprofitstep-2.png)\n\n##### 2 Import & Launch\n\nGet early access by setting up your next RSVP event or Ticketed Event. Or\nStart with a Donation Campaign.\n\n![](./img/shape-bg.png)![](img/nonprofitstep-3.png)\n\n##### 3 Engage & Grow\n\nLaunch Event, Membership or Donation campaigns with URLs for easy\ncontributions by all users via Web or Mobile app.\n\n## Engage from Anywhere    with FeatsClub\n\n### Stay connected on desktop or mobile. RSVP, donate, volunteer, or manage your community —  anytime, anywhere.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/ss1.png)\n\n![](./img/ss2.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/ss8.png)\n\n![](./img/ss9.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n###### DOWNLOAD APP ON\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n- [Privacy Policy](privacy-policy.html)\n- [Terms of Use](term-of-use.html)\n- [Terms for Organizations](terms-of-use-for-organizations.html)\n- [Parental Consent](parent-control.html)\n\n- [Funds Organizers](funds-organizers.html)\n- [Funds Donors](funds-donor.html)\n- [Ticket Policy](ticket-policy.html)\n\n- Chesterfield, Missouri, USA 63005\n- [![](./img/facebook.svg)](https://www.facebook.com/featsclub)[![](./img/instagram.svg)](https://www.instagram.com/featsclub)[![](./img/linkedin.svg)](https://www.linkedin.com/company/featsclub)\n\n\nDemo inquiries: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)\n\nPayments, refunds, and other queries:Please contact your organizers.",

      "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n# Simplify Membership & Fees. Strengthen Your Community.\n\nManage memberships and class or club fees seamlessly with online and offline payment options,\nplus lifetime, annual, or recurring plans.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/membership.png)\n\n## More Than Operations,  A Lifetime Engagement Platform.\n\nOne unified platform for enrollment, payments, compliance, communication, and recognition—turning\nparticipation into repeat support through memberships, events, micro-donations, and active cohorts.\n\n\n![](./img/m1.png)\n\n##### Flexible Plans & Audiences\n\nIndividual, family, student, volunteer, and\ncustom types; lifetime, annual, or recurring\nplans with built-in reminders.\n\n![](./img/m2.png)\n\n##### Compliance & Authentic  Records\n\nArchived history to comply with\nnonprofit statutes having auditable lists\nfor General Body Meeting, elections, and\nmember duties.\n\n![](./img/m3.png)\n\n##### Transparent, Streamlined  Payments\n\nLow commissions with a standard\nconvenience fee—no hidden add-ons or\ndouble-digit tips. Offline payments\nrecorded in the same ledger.\n\n#### Lower Costs, better experiences.\n\nNo Forced Tips & Exorbitant Fees. Premium Network tools\n\nto Build & Engage your Community for Life.\n\n\n## Everything You Need for Memberships     & Fees in One Place\n\n- ##### Plan Setups\n\n\n\nCreate lifetime, one-time annual, or recurring memberships; configure class/club fees\nsimilarly.\n\n- ##### Membership Types\n\n\n\nIndividual, family, student, volunteer, and custom tiers.\n\n- ##### Registration & Participation Forms\n\n\n\nCollect member and family data, preferences, and required information.\n\n- ##### Online & Offline Payments\n\n\n\nAccept secure online payments; record cash/check/external payments to keep a complete\nledger\n\n- ##### Digital Cards & Class Passes\n\n\n\nAuto-issue membership cards/passes via email or app for quick verification.\n\n- ##### Check ins & Access Control\n\n\n\nTag members for free/discounted event entry while guests are charged.\n\n\n![](./img/m11.png)\n\n## Easy to Set Up. Seamless to Manage.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/memberstep-1.png)\n\n##### 1 Set Up Plans & Fees\n\nDefine membership tiers and class/\nclub fee structures in minutes either\nin App or on the Web.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/memberstep-2.png)\n\n##### 2 Share Payment Links & Invite\n\nShare payment links via messaging or\nemail or your web sites, or invite\nfollowers directly in the app.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/memberstep-3.png)\n\n##### 3 Engage, Track & Retain\n\nIssue digital cards/passes, enable\ncheck ins, archive data for compliance,\nsend reminders, and keep members\ninformed.\n\n![](./img/q3.png)![](./img/q4.png)\n\n## Got Questions?\n\n## What is a membership on FeatsClub?\n\nA membership allows organizations to manage their members, collect dues, and provide exclusive access to events, content, and benefits. Members receive a digital membership card, can track their activities, and earn points and credentials through their participation.\n\n\n## Who can create memberships?\n\nOnly organizations can create and manage memberships. They configure tiers, pricing, benefits, and registration forms. Members join by purchasing or registering through the shared membership link.\n\n\n## How do I create a membership?\n\nMemberships are created through the FeatsClub mobile app:\n\n1. Navigate to the Memberships tab\n2. Tap 'Create Membership'\n3. Configure membership details (name, duration, pricing, benefits)\n4. Add custom registration form fields (optional)\n5. Save and share the membership link.\n\nMembers can join via web or via the FeatsClub app.\n\n## What membership durations are available?\n\nFeatsClub supports monthly, yearly, lifetime, and custom durations. Organizations can also set renewal reminders and grace periods.\n\n\n## What is a digital membership card?\n\nEach active member receives a digital membership card showing their name, photo, membership tier, validity dates, and a unique QR code for verification. Organizations can scan QR codes at events or venues.\n\n\n## Can I customize the membership registration form?\n\nYes. Organizations can add text fields, multiple choice questions, checkboxes, file uploads, and preset fields. All responses are securely stored and accessible in the member management dashboard.\n\n\n## How do I manage existing members?\n\nOrganizations can view active, expired, and pending members; search and filter lists; view member profiles; manually add or remove members; extend or cancel memberships; and export member data.\n\n\n## Can I offer member discounts for events?\n\nYes. Organizations can configure member-exclusive pricing for ticketed events. Discounts apply automatically, and member-only events can also be created.\n\n\n## Can members earn points and credentials?\n\nYes. Members earn points and verifiable credentials through volunteering, events, achievements, and donations. These appear in their FeatsClub portfolio and are shareable or verifiable.\n\n\n## What happens when a membership expires?\n\nExpired members lose access to member benefits and pricing. Their digital card shows 'Expired.' Members can renew anytime, and their activity history and credentials remain preserved.\n\n\n## Can I import existing members?\n\nYes. Organizations can prepare a spreadsheet with member details and contact appsupport@featsclub.com for imports. Members then receive activation invitations.\n\n\n## What are Fees in FeatsClub?\n\nFees enable organizations to collect payments for classes, programs, workshops, or any recurring or one-time payment. Fees are separate from memberships and can be assigned to individuals or groups.\n\n\n## How do I create and collect a fee?\n\n1. Navigate to the Fees section\n2. Create a new fee (name, amount, due date)\n3. Assign the fee to members or groups\n4. Members receive notifications and pay online\n5. Payment status is tracked in the dashboard.\n\n## Can I set up recurring fees?\n\nYes. Fees can be one-time or recurring (weekly, monthly, quarterly, or custom). Recurring fees generate automatically with reminders.\n\n\nStill have questions?\n\n[Ask FeatsAI ✨](coming-soon.html)\n\n## Engage from Anywhere    with FeatsClub\n\n### Stay connected on desktop or mobile. RSVP, donate, volunteer, or manage your community —  anytime, anywhere.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/ss1.png)\n\n![](./img/ss2.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/ss8.png)\n\n![](./img/ss9.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n###### DOWNLOAD APP ON\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n- [Privacy Policy](privacy-policy.html)\n- [Terms of Use](term-of-use.html)\n- [Terms for Organizations](terms-of-use-for-organizations.html)\n- [Parental Consent](parent-control.html)\n\n- [Funds Organizers](funds-organizers.html)\n- [Funds Donors](funds-donor.html)\n- [Ticket Policy](ticket-policy.html)\n\n- Chesterfield, Missouri, USA 63005\n- [![](./img/facebook.svg)](https://www.facebook.com/featsclub)[![](./img/instagram.svg)](https://www.instagram.com/featsclub)[![](./img/linkedin.svg)](https://www.linkedin.com/company/featsclub)\n\n\nDemo inquiries: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)\n\nPayments, refunds, and other queries:Please contact your organizers.",

      "markdown": "At FeatsClub, all donations and fundraising efforts are conducted exclusively by verified,\nregistered organizations on our platform. To promote a safe and transparent environment,\ndonors must adhere to the following guidelines:\n\n\n## Tax Deductibility & Nonprofit Verification\n\nFeatsClub does not verify the tax-exempt status of organizations on our platform.\nSome organizations may self-declare as a \"Nonprofit - Self-declared\" label may appear\non their donation page, but **this self-declaration is not a verification by FeatsClub**.\n\n\nBefore claiming a tax deduction for any donation made through FeatsClub, donors should:\n\n\n- Independently verify the organization's 501(c)(3) status at the [IRS\\\n   Tax Exempt Organization Search](https://apps.irs.gov/app/eos/).\n\n- Request an official tax receipt directly from the organization.\n\n- Consult a qualified tax advisor for any questions about deductibility.\n\n\n**FeatsClub does not issue tax receipts.** Any tax receipt a donor receives is issued\nby the organization, not by FeatsClub. FeatsClub is not responsible for the accuracy or\nvalidity of any tax claim made in connection with a donation.\n\n\nCurrency, eligibility, and deductibility rules vary by jurisdiction. Donors outside the\nUnited States should consult local tax authorities for guidance applicable to their\ncountry of residence.\n\n\n## Donor Responsibility\n\n- **Verification:** Donors must verify the organization they wish to support before\n   making any donations. Contributions should only be made to organizations the donor is\n   familiar with and whose purpose aligns with their values.\n\n- **Purpose Clarity:** Donors should ensure they understand the specific purpose of the\n   fundraising campaign and trust the organization conducting it. FeatsClub strongly\n   discourages donations to unfamiliar organizations or organizers.\n\n- **Due Diligence Reminder:** Donors are responsible for assessing the legitimacy and\n   mission of any organization they support on FeatsClub. FeatsClub encourages donors to\n   perform reasonable due diligence before donating.\n\n\n## Processing Charges and Deductions\n\n- **Donation Processing Charges:** All donations made to organizations on FeatsClub\n   are subject to processing charges, which cover FeatsClub’s operational costs and fees\n   from payment facilitators. These charges are transparently communicated to the\n   organizations as part of their fundraising campaign setup. Donors should be aware that\n   their donations reach the organization after deducting these charges.\n\n- **On-Behalf Campaigns:** Users may create campaigns on behalf of organizations,\n   provided they have obtained permission from the organization. Both FeatsClub and the\n   organization reserve the right to terminate any on-behalf campaign if it is found to\n   violate organizational or platform guidelines or to be misaligned with the\n   organization’s mission or goals.\n\n\n## FeatsClub's Role and Limited Liability\n\n- **Facilitation of Engagement:** FeatsClub serves as a platform to connect\n   organizations with their existing and potential followers, members, and supporters who\n   have a vested interest in the organization’s mission.\n\n- **Platform Liability:** FeatsClub is not involved in the direct management,\n   distribution, or oversight of funds raised on the platform. FeatsClub is not responsible\n   for any misused or misappropriated funds. Donors agree to hold FeatsClub harmless from\n   any disputes arising from donations made through the platform.\n\n\n## Dispute Resolution and Refund Policy\n\n- **Resolution through Organization:** Donors are encouraged to resolve any disputes\n   directly with the organization. FeatsClub may assist by facilitating contact but does\n   not guarantee or manage refunds.\n\n- **No Guarantee of Refunds:** FeatsClub does not offer refunds for donations. Donors\n   are advised to donate only if they fully trust the organization and understand that\n   donations are final.\n\n- **Dispute Assistance:** If a dispute cannot be resolved directly, FeatsClub may\n   facilitate mediation at its discretion but is not liable for outcomes or refunds.\n\n\n## Privacy and Data Security\n\n- **Confidentiality During Investigations:** FeatsClub will maintain the\n   confidentiality of all donor and organization information during any investigation, in\n   compliance with relevant data protection laws (such as GDPR or CCPA, if applicable).\n\n\n## Adherence to FeatsClub Terms of Use for Users\n\n- **User Terms of Use:** In addition to this Fundraising Policy, all donors and users\n   must adhere to FeatsClub’s **Terms of Use for Users**, agreed to upon registration.\n   Violations of these Terms of Use may result in account suspension or termination.\n\n\n## Modification of Policy\n\n- **Policy Updates:** FeatsClub reserves the right to modify this policy at any time.\n   If the policy is modified, FeatsClub will post the updated policy on the platform or\n   notify users directly. All donations made before the effective date of the modification\n   are subject to the previous policy terms.\n\n\n## Liability and Indemnification\n\n- **Indemnity:** By using FeatsClub’s platform, donors agree to waive FeatsClub’s\n   liability in relation to any misuse of donated funds. FeatsClub is a facilitation\n   platform and is not responsible for the fundraising activities or fund management\n   practices of registered organizations.",

      "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n# Fuel Your Mission with Community-Driven Donations\n\nEmpower patrons and supporters to launch their own sub-campaigns that direct funds straight to\nyour organization. Turn micro-donations, events, and impact updates into a loyal, thriving donor\ncommunity.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/donation1.png)\n\n## Not Just Fundraising. A Community Engine.\n\nMove beyond one-off appeals. With FeatsClub, you build a donor\ncommunity—patron-led micro-campaigns, event-integrated giving, transparent pricing, ongoing updates,\nand meaningful recognition.\n\n![](./img/f1.png)\n\n- ##### Transparent, Mission-First Pricing\n\n\nLow commissions with a standard convenience fee. No hidden add-ons or double-digit\n“tips.” Keep more to reinvest in your cause.\n\n- ##### Patron-Led Micro-Campaigns\n\n\nSupporters (students, parents, alumni, fans) can create their own campaign pages with\nproceeds going directly to your org—perfect for micro-donations and school/academy\ndrives.\n\n- ##### Community &Trust at Scale\n\n\nShare impact updates, recognize fundraisers, and use FeatsAI to answer donor questions\nabout mission, programs, and past use of funds—boosting confidence and repeat giving.\n\n\n\n#### Lower Costs, better experiences.\n\nNo Forced Tips & Exorbitant Fees. Premium Network tools\n\nto Build & Engage your Community for Life.\n\n\n## Everything You Need for Donations —    And More\n\n- ##### Organisation Campaign\n\n\n\nLaunch official donation pages with story, goals, and visuals.\n\n- ##### Patron Sub-Campaigns\n\n\n\nLet supporters spin up their own campaigns; funds route to your org.\n\n- ##### Micro-Donations\n\n\n\nEncourage “give any amount” moments—ideal for students and event audiences.\n\n- ##### Event-Integrated Giving\n\n\n\nAdd donation prompts to free or ticketed events to amplify reach.\n\n- ##### Receipts & Acknowledgments\n\n\n\nIssue donation receipts suitable for donor records; custom thank-you notes.\n\n- ##### FeatsAI Donor Assistant\n\n\n\nReal-time Q&A about how funds are used, impact to date, and program details.\n\n\n![](./img/g1.png)\n\n## Simple to Launch. Built to Grow.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/donatestep-1.png)\n\n##### 1 Set Up Your Campaign\n\nCreate an org campaign (or enable patron sub-campaigns). Define goals,\nstory, images, and donation tiers. Connect Stripe and publish.\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/donatestep-2.png)\n\n##### 2 Activate Your Community\n\nShare links/QR, invite students/alumni/patrons to raise micro-donations,\nand add donation prompts to events and ticket flows\n\n![](./img/donationshap.png)![](img/donatestep-3.png)\n\n##### 3 Sustain & Grow\n\nPost impact updates, recognize fundraisers with endorsements/certificates,\nenable recurring gifts, and convert donors to members.\n\n![](./img/coin.png)\n\n##### Compliance & Pricing Note\n\nCurrently supported via Stripe. Eligible nonprofits may access Stripe\nnonprofit processing discounts. FeatsClub uses low commissions with a standard convenience\nfee—no hidden fees or double-digit tips.\n\n![](./img/q3.png)![](./img/q4.png)\n\n## Got Questions?\n\n## What is a donation campaign on FeatsClub?\n\nA donation campaign enables organizations and their members to raise funds for causes, projects, or community initiatives. Organizations can create campaigns directly, while members can propose campaigns that require organization approval before going live.\n\n\n## Can students and members receive credentials for organizing campaigns?\n\nYes. FeatsClub allows organizations to issue verified endorsements and digital certificates to students and members who create or contribute to donation campaigns. These credentials highlight leadership, initiative, community impact, and fundraising achievements—useful for school portfolios, college applications, resumes, and volunteer records.\n\n\n## Do I need a bank account set up to create campaigns?\n\nYes. FeatsClub requires organizations to complete business verification and set up their payment profile before launching campaigns. This ensures authenticity and prevents delays or fund-holding issues common on other platforms.\n\n\n## Who can create a donation campaign?\n\nBoth organizations and members can create campaigns:\n\n- **Organizations:** Can create, publish, edit, and close campaigns directly.\n- **Members:** Can propose campaigns that must be approved by the organization.\n\n## How do I create a donation campaign?\n\nCampaign creation is done through the FeatsClub mobile app. Donors can contribute and receive updates through the web or app. Organizations can access campaign creation through the Manage Donations section in the app.\n\n\n## Can we provide periodic updates to donors and recognize them?\n\nYes, FeatsClub is not just an operational tool but a lifetime engagement platform. You can share updates on donation campaigns on the FeatsClub app and web. The members and students of the organization can see how the donation has been put to work to create impact. In the future, organizations can reward Feats coins that can be redeemable for events and other perks.\n\n\n## How does the approval process work for student or member-created campaigns?\n\n1. **Create & Save Draft** – Campaign remains private.\n2. **Submit for Approval** – Sent to the organization for review.\n3. **Pending Approval** – Editing is disabled during review.\n4. **Approved** – Campaign becomes active.\n5. **Rejected** – Member receives feedback and can revise.\n\n## Can I edit my campaign after it's live?\n\nYes. Organizations can edit any active campaign. Approved member campaigns are managed by the organization once live. Updates take effect immediately.\n\n\n## How do I close a donation campaign?\n\n**Organizations:**\n\n1. Open the campaign detail screen.\n2. Select 'Close Campaign.'\n3. Confirm closure.\n\n**Members:** Campaigns proposed by members must be closed by the organization. Contact your admin to close your campaign.\n\n## What happens when an organization closes a member's campaign?\n\nDonations stop immediately. The campaign card displays 'Organization closed the campaign.' Members see 'Your organization closed the campaign. Contact admin to reopen.'\n\n\n## How can I track donations to my campaign?\n\nActive campaigns provide real-time insights, including total funds raised, number of donors, progress toward goal, and donation history.\n\n\n## Can organizations modify member campaigns before approval?\n\nYes. Organizations can edit campaign details and approve with or without modifications. The campaign history will show relevant updates.\n\n\nStill have questions?\n\n[Ask FeatsAI ✨](coming-soon.html)\n\n## Engage from Anywhere    with FeatsClub\n\n### Stay connected on desktop or mobile. RSVP, donate, volunteer, or manage your community —  anytime, anywhere.\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n![](./img/ss1.png)\n\n![](./img/ss2.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/ss8.png)\n\n![](./img/ss9.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss3.png)\n\n![](./img/ss4.png)\n\n![](./img/ss5.png)\n\n![](./img/ss6.png)\n\n![](./img/ss7.png)\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n###### DOWNLOAD APP ON\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n- [Privacy Policy](privacy-policy.html)\n- [Terms of Use](term-of-use.html)\n- [Terms for Organizations](terms-of-use-for-organizations.html)\n- [Parental Consent](parent-control.html)\n\n- [Funds Organizers](funds-organizers.html)\n- [Funds Donors](funds-donor.html)\n- [Ticket Policy](ticket-policy.html)\n\n- Chesterfield, Missouri, USA 63005\n- [![](./img/facebook.svg)](https://www.facebook.com/featsclub)[![](./img/instagram.svg)](https://www.instagram.com/featsclub)[![](./img/linkedin.svg)](https://www.linkedin.com/company/featsclub)\n\n\nDemo inquiries: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)\n\nPayments, refunds, and other queries:Please contact your organizers.",
      "metadata": {
