      "markdown": "Last updated: June 18, 2024\n\n### **Introduction**\n\nFeatsClub Inc. ( **\"FeatsClub\"** or **\"We\"**) respect your\nprivacy and are committed to protecting it through our compliance with this Privacy\nStatement.\n\nThis Privacy Statement describes the types of Personal Information we may collect from you or\nthat you may provide when you use our application (the \" **App**\") and our\npractices for collecting, using, maintaining, protecting, and disclosing that information.\n\n\n“Personal Information” as used in this Statement means information that identifies, relates\nto, describes, or can reasonably be linked, directly or indirectly, to a specific natural\nperson. It does not include information that is considered anonymous, de-identified, or\naggregated by applicable law.\n\nThis Privacy Statement applies to the information we collect:\n\n- On the App\n- In email, text, and other electronic messages between you and FeatsClub.\n- When you interact with our advertising on third-party Apps and services, if those\n   applications or advertising include links to this Privacy Statement.\n\nPlease read this Privacy Statement carefully to understand our policies and practices\nregarding your information and how we will treat it. If you do not agree with our policies\nand practices, your choice is not to use our App. By accessing or using the App, you agree\nto this Privacy Statement. This Privacy Statement may change from time to time. Your\ncontinued use of the App after we make changes is deemed to be acceptance of those changes,\nso please check the Privacy Statement periodically for updates.\n\n### **Children Under the Age of 13**\n\nThe Children's Online Privacy Protection Act of 1998 and its rules (collectively, \"COPPA\")\nrequire us to inform parents and legal guardians (as used in this section,\n\" **parents**\") about our practices for collecting, using, and disclosing\npersonal information from children under the age of 13 (\" **children**\"). It\nalso requires us to obtain verifiable consent from a child's parent for certain collection,\nuse, and disclosure of the child's personal information.\n\nThis section notifies parents of:\n\n- The types of information we may collect from children.\n- How we use the information we collect.\n- Our practices for disclosing that information.\n- Our practices for notifying and obtaining parents' consent when we collect personal\n   information from children, including how a parent may revoke consent.\n- All operators that collect or maintain information from children through this FEATSCLUB\n   App.\n\nThis section only applies to children under the age of 13 and supplements the other\nprovisions of this privacy policy.\n\n### **Information We Collect from Children**\n\nApp and its content and features are only available to registered users. In addition, we use\ncertain technologies, such as cookies, to automatically collect information from our users\n(including children) when they visit or use the App.\n\nWe only collect as much information about a child as is reasonably necessary for the child to\nparticipate in an activity, and we do not condition his or her participation on the\ndisclosure of more personal information than is reasonably necessary.\n\n### **Information We Collect Directly**\n\n**.** A child must provide us with the following information to register with\nthe App:\n\n- Name\n- Gender\n- Photo\n- Email address\n- Phone number\n- Birthdate\n- Extracurricular interests, activities, skills, and achievements\n- A screen or user name that functions as online contact information\n\nWe also require the child to create a member name and password. We may request additional\ninformation from your child. We specify whether information is required or optional when we\nrequest it.\n\nWe enable registered users to communicate with others on or through this App through chat,\nand posting to public areas of the Service (\" **Social Features**\"). The nature\nof these features allows children to disclose personal information about themselves. We do\nnot monitor or review this content before it is posted, and we do not control the actions of\nthird parties with whom your child shares his or her information. We encourage parents to\neducate their children about safe internet use and to monitor their children's use of social\nfeatures.\n\n### **Automatic Information Collection and Tracking**\n\n**.** We use technology to automatically collect information from our users,\nincluding children when they access and navigate through the App and use certain of its\nfeatures. The information we collect through these technologies may include:\n\n- One or more persistent identifiers that can be used to recognize a user over time and\n   across different websites and online services.\n- Information that identifies a device's location (geolocation information).\n\nWe may combine non-personal information we collect through these technologies with personal\ninformation about you or your child that we collect online.\n\nFor information about our automatic information collection practices, including how you can\nopt out of certain information collection, see the \" Information We Collect Through\nAutomatic Data Collection Technologies,\" \"Choices About How We Use and Disclose Your\nInformation,” and “California & Other U.S. State Privacy Rights \" sections of our\ngeneral Privacy Policy.\n\n### **How We Use Your Child's Information**\n\nWe use the personal information we collect from your child to:\n\n- Register him or her with the Service;\n- Communicate with him or her about activities or features of the App that may be of\n   interest;\n- Track his or her performance in games or other activities;\n- Provide services he or she requested;\n- Market new academies or coaches, volunteering opportunities, or part-time roles to\n   children.\n\nWe use the information we collect automatically through technology (see Automatic Information\nCollection and Tracking) and other non-personal information we collect to improve our App\nand to deliver a better and more personalized experience by enabling us to:\n\n- Estimate our audience size and usage patterns.\n- Store information about the child's preferences, allowing us to customize the content\n   according to individual interests.\n- Speed up your searches.\n\nWe use geolocation information we collect to provide relevant information on local academies,\ncoaches, events, venues, and other information that will benefit users.\n\n### **Our Practices for Disclosing Children's Information**\n\nWe do not share, sell, rent, or transfer children's personal information other than as\ndescribed in this section.\n\nWe may disclose aggregated information about many of our users and information that does not\nidentify any individual or device. In addition, we may disclose children's personal\ninformation:\n\n- To third parties we use to support the internal operations of our App and who are bound\n   by contractual or other obligations to use the information only for such purpose and to\n   keep the information confidential.\n- If we are required to do so by law or legal process, such as to comply with any court\n   order or subpoena or to respond to any government or regulatory request.\n- If we believe disclosure is necessary or appropriate to protect the rights, property, or\n   safety of FeatsClub and our customers or others, including to:\n\n- protect the safety of a child;\n- protect the safety and security of the App; or\n- enable us to take precautions against liability.\n\n- To law enforcement agencies or for an investigation related to public safety.\n\nIn addition, if we are involved in a merger, divestiture, restructuring, reorganization,\ndissolution, or other sale or transfer of some or all of FeatsClub's assets, whether as a\ngoing concern or as part of bankruptcy, liquidation or similar proceeding or event, we may\ntransfer the personal information we have collected or maintain to the buyer or other\nsuccessor.\n\n### **Commitment to Child Safety**\n\nFeatsClub is dedicated to providing a safe environment for all users, especially children. We\nhave a zero-tolerance policy towards child sexual abuse and exploitation (CSAE) and are\ncommitted to preventing the presence and distribution of child sexual abuse material (CSAM)\non our platform.\n\n### **Compliance with Global Child Safety Laws**\n\nIn addition to COPPA, FeatsClub complies with all applicable child safety laws and\nregulations, including the UK's Age Appropriate Design Code and the EU's General Data\nProtection Regulation (GDPR) concerning children's data.\n\n### **Social Features**\n\n**.** The App offers social features, including chat and posting on message\nboards that enable children to communicate with others through the App. We do not control\nthe types of information your child can send or post through them. We encourage parents to\neducate their children about safety online and to carefully monitor their children's use of\nsocial features to ensure they do not disclose their personal information through them.\n\n### **Accessing and Correcting Your Child's Personal Information**\n\nAt any time, you may review the child's personal information maintained by us, require us to\ncorrect or delete the personal information, and/or refuse to permit us from further\ncollecting or using the child's information.\n\nYou can review, change, or delete your child's personal information by:\n\n- Logging into your child's account and visiting his or her account profile page.\n\n- Sending us an email at appsupport@featsclub.com. To protect your privacy and security,\n   we may require you to take certain steps or provide additional information to verify\n   your identity before we provide any information or make corrections.\n- For any child safety concerns or inquiries, please contact our operations team that\n   monitor content at appsupport@featsclub.com.\n\n### **In-App Reporting Mechanism**\n\nWe have implemented an in-app feature that allows users to report any inappropriate content\nor behavior. This tool enables users to flag content that may be harmful or violates our\ncommunity guidelines, ensuring prompt review and action by our moderation team.\n\n### **Procedures for Handling CSAM**\n\nUpon identification of any CSAM, we will:\n\n- Immediately remove the content from our platform.\n\n- Report the material to the appropriate law enforcement agencies and the National\n   Center for Missing & Exploited Children (NCMEC).\n\n- Cooperate fully with authorities during any investigations.\n\n\n### **Operators That Collect or Maintain Information from Children**\n\nA list of all operators that may collect or maintain personal information from children\nthrough the App is available _here_: Sertifier ( [www.sertifier.com](http://www.sertifier.com)), Stripe (www.stripe.com).\n\nPlease direct your inquiries about any operator's privacy practices and use of children's\ninformation to the operator at its contact information provided.\n\n### **Rights Under The Family Education Rights and Privacy Act (“FERPA”)**\n\nUnder certain circumstances, if you enroll in a program offered by one of our partnering\neducational or learning institutions, the information that we may receive from our\npartnering educational or learning institutions that pertain to you may be protected under\nFERPA, if applicable. You may also have specific rights related to accessing and/or updating\nyour personal information when FERPA applies. For more information related to the\napplicability of FERPA and your rights under it, please contact the partner educational or\nlearning institution in which you are or were enrolled.\n\n### **Information We Collect About You and How We Collect It**\n\nWe collect several types of information from and about users of our App, including\ninformation:\n\n- By which you may be personally identified, such as name, email address, telephone\n   number, date of birth, gender, or any other identifier by which you may be contacted\n   online or offline. (\" **Personal Information**\").\n- That is about you, such as skills, activities, and certificates; and/or\n- About your internet connection, the equipment you use to access our App, and usage\n   details.\n\nWe collect this information:\n\n- Directly from you when you provide it to us.\n- Automatically as you navigate through the App. Information collected automatically may\n   include usage details, IP addresses, and information collected through cookies, web\n   quickcrawls, and other tracking technologies.\n- From third parties from whom you authorize us to collect data\n\n_Information You Provide to Us_\n\nThe information we collect on or through our App may include:\n\n- Information that you provide by filling in forms on our App. This includes information\n   provided at the time of registering to use our App, subscribing to our service, posting\n   material, or requesting further services. We may also ask you for information when you\n   enter a contest or promotion sponsored by us, and when you report a problem with our\n   App.\n- Records and copies of your correspondence (including email addresses), if you contact\n   us.\n- Your responses to surveys that we might ask you to complete for research purposes.\n- Details of transactions you carry out through our App and of the fulfillment of your\n   orders. You may be required to provide financial information before placing an order\n   through our App.\n\nYou also may provide information to be published or displayed (hereinafter,\n\" **posted**\") on public areas of the App, or transmitted to other users of the\nApp or third parties (collectively, \" **User Contributions**\"). Your User\nContributions are posted on and transmitted to others at your own risk. Please be aware that\nno security measures are perfect or impenetrable. Additionally, we cannot control the\nactions of other users of the App with whom you may choose to share your User Contributions.\nTherefore, we cannot and do not guarantee that your User Contributions will not be viewed by\nunauthorized persons.\n\n_Information We Collect Through Automatic Data Collection_\n_Technologies_\n\nAs you navigate through and interact with our App, we may use automatic data collection\ntechnologies to collect certain information about your equipment, browsing actions, and\npatterns, including:\n\n-  Details of your visits to our App, including traffic data, location data, logs,\n   and other communication data and the resources that you access and use on the App.\n- Information about your computer and internet connection, including your IP address,\n   operating system, and browser type.\n\nWe also may use these technologies to collect information about your online activities over\ntime and across third-party Apps or other online services, including your behavior data to\nhelp surface relevant skills related information and services. Most web browsers and some\nmobile operating systems and mobile applications include a Do-Not-Track (\"DNT\") feature or\nsetting you can activate to signal your privacy preference not to have data about your\nonline browsing activities monitored and collected. At this stage, no uniform technology\nstandard for recognizing and implementing DNT signals has been finalized. As such, we do not\ncurrently respond to DNT browser signals or any other mechanism that automatically\ncommunicates your choice not to be tracked online. If a standard for online tracking is\nadopted that we must follow in the future, we will inform you about that practice in a\nrevised version of this privacy notice.\n\nThe information we collect automatically may include Personal Information, or we may maintain\nit or associate it with Personal Information we collect in other ways or receive from third\nparties. It helps us to improve our App and to deliver a better and more personalized\nservice, including by enabling us to:\n\n- Estimate our audience size and usage patterns.\n- Store information about your preferences, allowing us to customize our App according to\n   your individual interests.\n- Speed up your searches.\n- Recognize you when you return to our App.\n\nThe technologies we use for this automatic data collection may include:\n\n- **Cookies (or browser cookies).** A cookie is a small file placed on the\n   hard drive of your computer. You may refuse to accept browser cookies by activating the\n   appropriate setting on your browser. However, if you select this setting you may be\n   unable to access certain parts of our App. Unless you have adjusted your browser setting\n   so that it will refuse cookies, our system will issue cookies when you direct your\n   browser to our App.\n- **Web quickcrawls.** Pages of our App and our emails may contain small\n   electronic files known as web quickcrawls (also referred to as clear gifs, pixel tags, and\n   single-pixel gifs) that permit FeatsClub, for example, to count users who have visited\n   those pages or opened an email and for other related App statistics (for example,\n   recording the popularity of certain App content and verifying system and server\n   integrity).\n- **Flash Cookies.** Certain features of our App may use local stored objects\n   (or Flash cookies) to collect and store information about your preferences and\n   navigation to, from, and on our App. Flash cookies are not managed by the same browser\n   settings as are used for browser cookies. For information about managing your privacy\n   and security settings for Flash cookies, see [Choices\\\n   About How We Use and Disclose Your Information](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1JeZD8uwGi3sHgjIVKtgS41cqRi2K6S5E/edit#bookmark=id.1t3h5sf).\n\nWe may tie information we collect through automatic data collection technologies to Personal\nInformation about you that we collect from other sources or you provide to us.\n\n### **Third-Party Use of Cookies and Other Tracking Technologies**\n\nSome content or applications, including advertisements, on the App are served by\nthird-parties, including advertisers, ad networks and servers, content providers, and\napplication providers. These third parties may use cookies alone or in conjunction with web\nquickcrawls or other tracking technologies\\] to collect information about you when you use our\nApp. The information they collect may be associated with your Personal Information or they\nmay collect information, including Personal Information, about your online activities over\ntime and across different Apps and other online services. They may use this information to\nprovide you with interest-based (behavioral) advertising or other targeted content.\n\n\nWe do not control these third parties' tracking technologies or how they may be used. If you\nhave any questions about an advertisement or other targeted content, you should contact the\nresponsible provider directly. For information about how you can opt out of receiving\ntargeted advertising from many providers, see [Choices\\\nAbout How We Use and Disclose Your Information](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1JeZD8uwGi3sHgjIVKtgS41cqRi2K6S5E/edit#bookmark=id.1t3h5sf).\\]\n\n### **How We Use Your Information**\n\nWe use information that we collect about you or that you provide to us, including any\nPersonal Information:\n\n- To present our App and their contents to you.\n- To provide you with information, products, or services that you request from us.\n- To fulfill any other purpose for which you provide it.\n- To provide you with notices about your account/subscription, including expiration and\n   renewal notices.\n- To carry out our obligations and enforce our rights arising from any contracts entered\n   into between you and us, including for billing and collection.\n- To notify you about changes to our App or any products or services we offer or provide\n   though it.\n- To allow you to participate in interactive features on our App.\n- In any other way we may describe when you provide the information.\n- For any other purpose with your consent.\n\nWe may also use your information to contact you about our own and third-parties' goods and\nservices that may be of interest to you. If you do not want us to use your information in\nthis way, please adjust your user preferences in your Profile and Account Management\nsections of App Settings. For more information, see Choices About How We Use and Disclose\nYour Information.\n\nWe may use the information we have collected from you to enable us to display advertisements\nto our advertisers' target audiences. Even though we do not disclose your Personal\nInformation for these purposes without your consent, if you click on or otherwise interact\nwith an advertisement, the advertiser may assume that you meet its target criteria.\n\nTo the extent permitted by applicable law, we also collect, create, process and store\ninformation that is not Personal Information such as sets of aggregate data and other\nde-identified Personal Information that does not reveal your identity. We retain the right\nto use and disclose information that is not Personal Information in any manner permitted by\nlaw.\n\n### **Disclosure of Your Information**\n\nWe may disclose Personal Information that we collect or you provide as described in this\nPrivacy Statement:\n\n- To contractors, service providers, and other third parties we use to support our\n   business and who are bound by contractual obligations to keep Personal Information\n   confidential and use it only for the purposes for which we disclose it to them.\n- To a buyer or other successor in the event of a merger, divestiture, restructuring,\n   reorganization, dissolution, or other sale or transfer of some or all of the FeatsClub’s\n   assets, whether as a going concern or as part of bankruptcy, liquidation, or similar\n   proceeding, in which Personal Information held by FeatsClub about our App users is among\n   the assets transferred.\n- To third parties to market their products or services to you if you have not opted out\n   of these disclosures. We contractually require these third parties to keep Personal\n   Information confidential and use it only for the purposes for which we disclose it to\n   them. For more information, see Choices About How We Use and Disclose Your Information.\n\n- To fulfill the purpose for which you provide it. For example, if you give us an email\n   address to use the “connect with organization or expert” or similar collaboration\n   feature of our App, we will transmit the contents of that email and your email address\n   to the recipients.\n- For any other purpose disclosed by us when you provide the information.\n- With your consent.\n\nWe may also disclose your Personal Information:\n\n- To comply with any court order, law, or legal process, including to respond to any\n   government or regulatory request.\n- To enforce or apply our terms of use [https://www.featsclub.com/termsofuse](https://www.featsclub.com/termsofuse)\n   and other agreements, including for billing and collection purposes.\n- If we believe disclosure is necessary or appropriate to protect the rights, property, or\n   safety of FeatsClub, our customers, or others. This includes exchanging information with\n   other companies and organizations for the purposes of fraud protection and credit risk\n   reduction.\n\n### **Choices About How We Use and Disclose Your Information**\n\nWe strive to provide you with choices regarding the Personal Information you provide to us.\nWe have created mechanisms to provide you with the following control over your\ninformation:\n\n- **Tracking Technologies and Advertising.** You can set your browser to\n   refuse all or some browser cookies, or to alert you when cookies are being sent. To\n   learn how you can manage your Flash cookie settings, visit the Flash player settings\n   page on Adobe's App. If you disable or refuse cookies, please note that some parts of\n   this site may then be inaccessible or not function properly.\n- **Disclosure of Your Information for Third-Party Advertising.** If you do\n   not want us to share your Personal Information with unaffiliated or non-agent third\n   parties for promotional purposes, you can opt-out by checking the relevant box in\n   Profile and Account Management sections of App Settings, or by sending us an email with\n   your request to appsupport@featsclub.com.\n- **Promotional Offers from the FeatsClub.** If you do not wish to have your\n   \\[email address/contact information\\] used by the FeatsClub to promote our own or third\n   parties' products or services, you can opt-out by or at any other time by checking the\n   relevant box in Profile and Account Management sections of App Settings, or by sending\n   us an email with your request to appsupport@featsclub.com. If we have sent you a\n   promotional email, you may send us a return email asking to be omitted from future email\n   distributions. This opt out does not apply to information provided to the FeatsClub as a\n   result of a product purchase, product service experience or other transactions.\n- **Targeted Advertising.** If you do not want us to use information that we\n   collect or that you provide to us to deliver advertisements according to our\n   advertisers' target-audience preferences, you can opt-out by \\[OPT-OUT METHOD\\]. \\[For this\n   opt-out to function, you must have your browser set to accept all browser\n   cookies.\\]\\]\n\nWe do not control third parties' collection or use of your information to serve\ninterest-based advertising. However, these third parties may provide you with ways to choose\nnot to have your information collected or used in this way. You can opt out of receiving\ntargeted ads from members of the Network Advertising Initiative (\" **NAI**\") on\nthe NAI's App.\n\nResidents of certain states, such as California, Nevada, Colorado, Connecticut, Virginia, and\nUtah may have additional Personal Information rights and choices. Please see sections below\nfor more information.\n\n### **Accessing and Correcting Your Information**\n\nYou can review and change your Personal Information by logging into the App or the\nApplication and visiting your account profile page.\n\nYou may also send us an email at appsupport@featsclub.com to request access to, correct or\ndelete any Personal Information that you have provided to us. We cannot delete your Personal\nInformation except by also deleting your user account. We may not accommodate a request to\nchange information if we believe the change would violate any law or legal requirement or\ncause the information to be incorrect.\n\nIf you delete your User Contributions from the App, copies of your User Contributions may\nremain viewable in cached and archived pages, or might have been copied or stored by other\nApp users.\n\nResidents of certain states, such as California, Nevada, Colorado, Virginia, Connecticut and\nUtah may have additional Personal Information rights and choices. Please see the sections\nbelow for more information.\n\n### **California & Other U.S. States Notice at Collection**\n\nFeatsClub may collect the following categories of personal data.\n\n**Category**\n\n**Examples**\n\n**Collected**\n\nA. Identifiers.\n\nA real name, alias, unique personal identifier, online identifier, Internet\nProtocol address, email address, account name, or other similar identifiers.\n\n\nYES\n\nB. Personal information categories listed in the California Customer Records\nstatute (Cal. Civ. Code 1798.80(e)).\n\nA name, signature, telephone number, bank account number, credit card number,\ndebit card number, or any other financial information,.\n\nSome Personal Information included in this category may overlap with other\ncategories.\n\nYES\n\nF. Internet or other similar network activity.\n\nBrowsing history, search history, information on a consumer's interaction\nwith a App, application, or advertisement.\n\nYES\n\nG. Geolocation data.\n\nPhysical location or movements.\n\nYES\n\nK. Inferences drawn from other Personal Information.\n\nProfile reflecting a person's preferences, characteristics, psychological\ntrends, predispositions, behavior, attitudes, intelligence, abilities, and\naptitudes.\n\nYES\n\nSources: We receive information from sources as described in the “Information We Collect\nAbout You and How We Collect It” section.\n\nFeatsClub’s business and commercial purposes for use: FeatsClub uses personal data for the\nfollowing business and commercial purposes: to provide FeatsClub services; for product\nresearch and development; for marketing and promotions; authentication, integrity, security,\nand safety; to communicate with you; and for legal reasons. For more information, please see\n“How We Share Personal Data?”\n\nCategories of third parties to whom we disclose Personal Information for business purposes\nare described in “Disclosure of Your Information”.\n\nFeatsClub may permit advertising and analytics services that are intended to deliver\nadvertising to you and/or analyze your interactions, based on your interactions with our App\nor app which may constitute a “sale” or “sharing” of data for targeted advertising purposes\nunder certain state privacy laws. See “California & Other U.S. State Privacy Rights” for\nmore information regarding your right to opt-out.\n\nRetention: FeatsClub retains personal data for as long as required to engage in the uses\ndescribed in this Privacy Statement, unless a longer retention period is required by\napplicable law or is advisable in light of our legal position (such as in regard to the\nenforcement of our agreements, the resolution of disputes, and applicable statutes of\nlimitations, litigation, or regulatory investigation).\n\n### **California & Other U.S. State Privacy Rights**\n\nUnder some U.S. state laws, including the California Consumer Privacy Act of 2018 (as amended\nby the California Consumer Privacy Rights Act) (CCPA), residents may have a right to:\n\nAccess the categories and specific pieces of personal data FeatsClub has collected, the\ncategories of sources from which the personal data is collected, the business purpose(s) for\ncollecting the personal data, and the categories of third parties with whom FeatsClub has\nshared personal data, and obtain the personal data in a portable and, to the extent\ntechnically feasible, readily usable format;\n\nDelete personal data under certain circumstances;\n\nCorrect personal data under certain circumstances; and\n\nOpt out of the “sale” of personal data or “sharing” of personal data for targeted advertising\npurposes. We do not sell your personal data in the conventional sense. However, like many\ncompanies, we may use advertising and analytics services that are intended to analyze your\ninteractions with our App or app, based on information obtained from cookies or other\ntrackers, including for delivering advertising to you (such as interest-based, targeted, or\ncross-context behavioral advertising). You can get more information and opt out of the use\nof cookies and other trackers on our App by changing settings in Profile and Account\nManagement sections of App Settings, or by sending us an email with your request to\nappsupport@featsclub.com. You will need to set your preferences from each device and each\nweb browser from which you wish to opt out. This feature uses a cookie to remember your\npreference, so if you clear all cookies from your browser, you will need to re-select your\npreferred settings. California and Connecticut residents may also set the Global Privacy\nControl (GPC) to opt out of the “sale” or “sharing” of your Personal Information for\ntargeted advertising for each participating browser system that you use. FeatsClub does not\nhave actual knowledge that it “sells” or “shares” the Personal Information of consumers\nunder 16 years of age.\n\nAppeal a denial of your request. Some states provide additional rights to their residents. If\nwe decline to process your request, you may have the right to appeal our decision. You can\ndo so by replying directly to our denial or emailing appsupport@featsclub.com.\n\nFeatsClub will not discriminate against you for exercising any of these rights, which is\nfurther in line with your rights under state law.\n\n### **Sensitive Information**\n\n. FeatsClub receives information that may be considered sensitive under some state laws, such\nas certain Account Information (e.g., financial information, log-in information). FeatsClub\nprocesses sensitive Personal Information to provide FeatsClub services, for product research\nand development, for authentication, integrity, security, and safety reasons, to communicate\nwith you, for legal reasons, and with your consent. FeatsClub does not use or disclose\nsensitive Personal Information (as defined under CCPA) for purposes of inferring\ncharacteristics about a consumer, or in any way that would require FeatsClub to provide a\nright to limit under the CCPA. Under certain laws, residents may also be permitted to opt\nout of certain profiling relating to automated processing analyzing certain categories of an\nindividual’s information that would produce a legal or similarly significant effect.\nFeatsClub does not engage in this type of profiling of individuals.\n\nTo exercise your rights, you can either contact us using the contact details provided under\nthe “Contact Information” heading below or using the Form by submitting the required\nverifying information. To opt out of the use of cookies on our sites for interest-based\nadvertising purposes, follow the instructions above.\n\nWe will acknowledge receipt of your request within 10 business days, and provide a\nsubstantive response within 45 calendar days, or inform you of the reason and extension\nperiod (up to a total of 90 days) in writing.\n\nThese rights are not absolute, are subject to exceptions and limitations, and may not be\nafforded to residents of all states. In certain cases, we may decline requests to exercise\nthese rights where permitted by law. We will need to verify your identity to process your\naccess, deletion, and correction requests and reserve the right to confirm your state\nresidency. To verify your identity, we may require you to log into your existing FeatsClub\naccount (if applicable), give a declaration as to your identity under penalty of perjury,\nand/or provide additional information, such as providing at least two pieces of Personal\nInformation relating to your account (which will be compared to information we have, such as\nprofile information) or as we otherwise may already have in our possession, such as your\nemail address and phone number. We will verify your consumer request by comparing the\ninformation you provide to information already in our possession, and take additional steps\nto minimize the risk of fraud. You may designate an authorized agent to submit your verified\nconsumer request by providing written permission and verifying your identity, or through\nproof of power of attorney.\n\nCalifornia’s Shine the Light Law\n\nCalifornia Civil Code Section 1798.83, also known as “Shine The Light” law, permits\nCalifornia residents to annually request information regarding the disclosure of your\nPersonal Information (if any) to third parties for the third parties’ direct marketing\npurposes in the preceding calendar year. We do not share Personal Information with third\nparties for the third parties’ direct marketing purposes.\n\nVirginia Consumer Data Protection Act De-identified Data Disclosure.  We may use\nde-identified data in some instances.  We either maintain such data without attempting\nto re-identify it or treat such data as personal data subject to applicable law. We will\nonly use personal information provided in your request to verify your identity or authority\nto make the request. To the extent possible, we will avoid requesting additional information\nfrom you for the purposes of verification. However, if we cannot verify your identity from\nthe information already maintained by us, we may request that you provide additional\ninformation for the purposes of verifying your identity and for security or fraud-prevention\npurposes. We will delete such additionally provided information as soon as we finish\nverifying you.\n\n### **Data Security**\n\nWe have implemented measures designed to secure your Personal Information from accidental\nloss and from unauthorized access, use, alteration, and disclosure. All information you\nprovide to us is stored on our secure servers behind firewalls. Any payment transactions and\nrelated information will be encrypted.\n\nThe safety and security of your information also depend on you. Where we have given you (or\nwhere you have chosen) a password for access to certain parts of our App and Application,\nyou are responsible for keeping this password confidential. We ask you not to share your\npassword with anyone. We urge you to be careful about giving out information in public areas\nof the App like message boards. The information you share in public areas may be viewed by\nany user of the App.\n\nUnfortunately, the transmission of information via the Internet is not completely secure.\nAlthough we do our best to protect your Personal Information, we cannot guarantee the\nsecurity of your Personal Information transmitted to our App or Application. Any\ntransmission of Personal Information is at your own risk. We are not responsible for the\ncircumvention of any privacy settings or security measures contained on the App or\nApplication.\n\n### **Changes to Our Privacy Statement**\n\nIt is our Privacy Statement to post any changes we make to our Privacy Statement on this\npage. If we make material changes to how we treat our users' Personal Information, we will\nnotify you either by email to the email address specified in your account or through a\nnotice on the App home page, or through push messaging. The date the Privacy Statement was\nlast revised is identified at the top of the page. You are responsible for ensuring we have\nan up-to-date active and deliverable email address for you, and for periodically visiting\nour App and this Privacy Statement to check for any changes.\n\n### **Contact Information**\n\nTo ask questions or comment about this Privacy Statement and our privacy practices or express\nyour concerns, please contact us at: appsupport@featsclub.com or +1 314 629 9754.\n\nIf you have complaints about our processing of your personal information, you may have the\nright to contact the applicable supervisory authority.",

      "markdown": "Last updated: June 18, 2024\n\n### **Parent Consent**\n\nIn order to comply with the federal law known as COPPA (Children’s Online Privacy Protection Act),\nFeatsClub Inc. (“we”, “us”, “our” or “ours”) is required to obtain prior parental consent to\ncollect, use and disclose personal information (“Personal Data”) from children under the age of 13\nwho wish to access and use our FeatsClub application (the “App”).\n\nWe have collected your contact information from your child in order to obtain your consent. As part\nof your child’s FeatsClub account, we may collect Personal Data from your child online. This\ninformation may be collected by us or by a third party to:\n\n- Register your child with the App;\n- Communicate with your child about activities or features of the App that may be of interest;\n\n- Track your child’s performance in games or other activities;\n- Provide services your child requests;\n- Market new academies or coaches, volunteering opportunities, or part-time roles to your child.\n\n\nWe will not collect, use or disclose any Personal Data from children under 13 years of age without\nparental consent.  We may collect the following Personal Data from your child once they are\nable to access the App:\n\n- Name\n- Gender\n- Photo\n- Email address\n- Phone number\n- Birthdate\n- Extracurricular interests, activities, skills, and achievements\n- Associated school clubs or community service organizations\n- A screen or user name that functions as online contact information\n- Social handle links\n- Event participation interests and attendance\n- Interactions within the App, such as posts, chats and purchases\n- Geolocation data\n- Persistent identifiers\n\nWith parental consent to our collection of information from your child, we may share or disclose such\ninformation in the following instances:\n\n- We may share information with our service providers, if necessary, to perform a business,\n   professional, or technology support function for us;\n- We may disclose Personal Data if permitted or required by law;\n- We may disclose Personal Data If we believe disclosure is necessary or appropriate to protect\n   the rights, property, or safety of FeatsClub and our customers or others\n\nPlease refer to our Policy Statement for details about our data privacy practices. You can find the\nonline Policy Statement www.featsclub.com/privacy-policy. At any time, you may refuse to permit us\nto collect further Personal Data from your child online.  You can request that we delete the\nPersonal Data we have collected in connection with your child’s account. A request to delete records\nmay lead to your child being unable to access our App.\n\nYou can review, change, or delete your child's Personal Data by:\n\n· Logging into your child's account and visiting his or her account profile page.\n\n· Sending us an email at contact@featsclub.com\n\nTo protect your privacy and security, we may require you to take certain steps or provide additional\ninformation to verify your identity before we provide any information or make corrections.\n\nOnce you have reviewed our online Privacy Statement www.featsclub.com/privacy-policy, you may provide\nyour consent to our collection, use, or disclosure of your child’s Personal Data by indicating so in\nour App during sign up and performing a verifiable financial transaction.\n\nIf you do not provide consent within 30 days from the date the direct notice was sent, we will delete\nyour email address from our records.\n\nPlease feel free to contact us with any questions or concerns at contact@featsclub.com.",

      "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n![](./img/esr1.png)\n\n# Employee Engagement & CSR Platform\n\nA comprehensive platform to track volunteering, manage internal events, recognize achievements\nbeyond work, and build authentic engagement that highlights leadership and community impact.\n\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n## FeatsClub Corporate Solutions\n\nA modern platform designed for enterprise environments—empowering employee engagement\n\nwhile meeting corporate security standards and delivering\nmeasurable CSR outcomes.\n\n![](./img/m4.png)\n\n##### Employee Volunteering &  CSR Impact\n\nTrack volunteer hours and community projects, measure real impact, and generate\nstakeholder-ready reports—all in one streamlined system.\n\n![](./img/m5.png)\n\n##### Internal Events &  Innovation Management\n\nManage hackathons, conferences, and innovation challenges with streamlined tracking that\ndrives participation and outcomes.\n\n![](./img/m6.png)\n\n##### Employee Recognition  & Development\n\nDocument patents, publications, and leadership to showcase growth and strengthen records\nfor reviews and promotions.\n\n## Key Features\n\n##### Volunteering & CSR Impact Management\n\n- Track volunteer hours across initiatives\n- Coordinate team volunteering and service projects\n- Generate CSR & sustainability reports for stakeholders\n- Match employees with meaningful opportunities\n- Measure community impact and engagement\n\n![](./img/corporatefeature-1.png)\n\n![](./img/corporatefeature-2.png)\n\n##### Innovation Events & Challenges\n\n- Organize internal hackathons and challenges\n- Track participation, teams, and outcomes\n- Foster cross-department collaboration\n- Document IP and patents\n- Showcase innovation culture externally\n\n##### Employee Networks & BRGs\n\n- Manage affinity groups and BRGs\n- Facilitate events and initiatives\n- Track membership and engagement\n- Support DEI programs\n- Enable networking and mentorship\n\n![](./img/corporatefeature-3.png)\n\n![](./img/corporatefeature-4.png)\n\n##### Authentic Recognition & Development\n\n- Document patents, publications, and achievements\n- Track speaking engagements and leadership activities\n- Recognize customer success and innovation contributions\n- Build employee accomplishment profiles\n- Strengthen performance reviews and promotions\n\n##### Enterprise Event Management\n\n- Manage conferences, all-hands, and events\n- Handle registrations, check-ins, and attendance\n- Manage speakers and sessions\n- Enable networking and professional development\n- Generate event engagement analytics\n\n![](./img/corporatefeature-5.png)\n\n![](./img/corporatefeature-6.png)\n\n##### CSR Analytics & Reporting\n\n- Comprehensive Employee and Social impact dashboards\n- Track sustainability and community outcomes\n- Provide executive-level engagement metrics\n\n## Platform Highlights\n\nBuilt for Enterprise Security, Scale, and Integration\n\n##### Enterprise Security\n\nSOC 2 compliant with SSO, encryption, and audit trails.\n\n##### Integration Capabilities\n\nConnect with HRIS, Slack, Teams, and corporate systems.\n\n##### Compliance & Governance\n\nGDPR and corporate policy adherence with audit features.\n\n![Platform Highlights](./img/schoolheighlight1.png)\n\n##### Advanced Analytics & Reporting\n\nDashboards for CSR and engagement insights.\n\n##### Scalable Architecture\n\nSupports thousands of employees with high availability.\n\n##### Single or Multi-Tenant Architecture\n\nTailored user experiences with corporate branding.\n\n![Platform Highlights](./img/gsg1.png)\n\n##### Enterprise Security\n\nSOC 2 compliant with SSO, encryption, and audit trails.\n\n##### Integration Capabilities\n\nConnect with HRIS, Slack, Teams, and corporate systems.\n\n##### Compliance & Governance\n\nGDPR and corporate policy adherence with audit features.\n\n##### Advanced Analytics & Reporting\n\nDashboards for CSR and engagement insights.\n\n##### Scalable Architecture\n\nSupports thousands of employees with high availability.\n\n##### Single or Multi-Tenant Architecture\n\nTailored user experiences with corporate branding.\n\n## Enterprise Implementation in Weeks, Not Months\n\nBuilt for Enterprise Security, Scale, and Integration\n\n![](./img/shape-bg.png)![](img/corporatestep-1.png)\n\n##### 1 Discovery & Configuration\n\nConsultation to align with CSR\ngoals and configure Platform to\ntrack activities that matter.\n\n![](./img/shape-bg.png)![](img/corporatestep-2.png)\n\n##### 2 Integration & Migration\n\nIntegrate with Corporate on\ndemand for employee\nleadership and contributions.\n\n![](./img/shape-bg.png)![](img/corporatestep-3.png)\n\n##### 3 Launch & Scale\n\nPilot, train HR and engagement\nleads, then scale organization\nwide.\n\n## Contact FeatsClub for Our team\nwill contact you soon.\n\n\nCompany Name \\*\n\nPlease enter the company name.\n\nContact Person \\*\n\nPlease enter the contact person name.\n\nTitle/Role \\*Select TitleCEO/FounderHR DirectorHR ManagerCSR ManagerEmployee Engagement LeadIT DirectorOperations ManagerOther\n\nPlease select a title/role.\n\nEmail Address \\*\n\nPlease enter a valid email address.\n\nPhone Number\n\nPlease enter a valid phone number.\n\nCompany Size Select Size1-50 employees51-200 employees201-500 employees501-1000 employees1001-5000 employees5000+ employees\n\nCurrent Employee Engagement Tools\n\nCSR & Volunteering Priorities\n\nPlease complete the CAPTCHA verification.\n\nSchedule an Enterprise Demo\n\n## Engage from Anywhere    with FeatsClub\n\n### Stay connected on desktop or mobile.       "markdown": "About Us - FeatsClub\n\n[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- Users  - [Students](student.html)\n  - [Community Members](community-members-and-users.html)\n- Organization  - [Organization](organization.html)\n  - [Events](event.html)\n  - [Donations](donation.html)\n  - [Membership](memberships-and-fees.html)\n  - [Clubs and Cohorts](classess-and-clubs.html)\n  - [FeatsAI](coming-soon.html)\n- Our Solutions  - [Schools & Universities](school.html)\n  - [Nonprofits & Community\\\n     Organizations](non-profit-org.html)\n  - [Extracurricular\\\n     Academies](extracurricular-academies.html)\n  - [Corporates &\\\n     Enterprises](corporate-employee-engagement.html)\n- About  - [About Us](about.html)\n  - [Our Ambassadors](our-ambassador.html)\n  - [Support](support.html)\n  - [Blog](blog.html)\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n![Build Your Network](./img/join-cta-logo.png)\n\n### **FeatsClub** voted No. 1 startup by St. Louis community in Startup World  Cup!\n\n![Showcase Skills](./img/join-cta-logo2.png)\n\n### **FeatsClub** is the ultimate hub for all extracurricular and community  activities\n\n![Showcase Skills](./img/join-cta-logo3.png)\n\n### **FeatsAI** is your one stop assistant guiding on your skill areas\n\n![Join FeatsClub](./img/join-cta-logo4.png)\n\n### Get your portfolio verified by anyone on **Feats Blockchain Network**\n\n![](./img/about-video.jpg)\n\n## Our North Star is to empower everyday achievers for a better world\n\n## Our Story\n\nWelcome to FeatsClub, where your journey begins, and possibilities are endless!\n\nWe are a people-focused technology company with a global vision. Our founders bring years of\nexperience building user-friendly technology solutions for corporations, leading not-for-profit\ncommunities for social impact, and advising aspiring entrepreneurs and students.\n\nMillions of individuals worldwide, from students to seasoned professionals, are eager to\npursue their passions and expand their horizons. Our mission is to empower them to build\npurposeful networks, showcase their talents, and explore new opportunities in diverse fields,\nsuch as education, sports, social service, and the arts.\n\nThousands of local communities worldwide, including coaches, gurus, clubs, mentors, and\nacademies, need a unified platform to share their journeys and societal contributions. At\nFeatsClub, we bridge this gap using innovative technology, including AI and Blockchain, coupled\nwith the power of purposeful collaboration.\n\nWe aim to redefine success, emphasizing the impact you make on society, not just\nthe number of followers you have. Join us in creating a better world for all, where every\nindividual's influence matters.\n\n### Our Mission\n\nBuild a global network connecting individuals and organizations to showcase, validate,\nenhance, and benefit from their skills and achievements.\n\n### Our Vision\n\nCreate a world of endless possibilities for all by organizing and empowering people's skills,\npassions, and achievements.\n\n## Meet Our Team\n\n### Passionate innovators dedicated to empowering everyday achievers\n\n![](./img/spn.jpeg)\n\n#### Nagendra SP\n\nChief Executive Officer, USA\n\nInterested in solving everyday problems through technology. Served in IT leadership\nroles for large enterprises building platforms and products at scale. Passionate\ncitizen interested in promoting community activities and mentoring students.\n\n#### Nagendra SP\n\nChief Executive Officer, USA\n\n![](./img/prasanna4.jpeg)\n\n#### Prasanna Hegde\n\nChief Technology Officer, India\n\nPassionate technologist with rich experience in co-founding and guiding start ups.\nPracticing cloud architect with 3 decades of experience leading architecture\npractices.\n\n#### Prasanna Hegde\n\nChief Technology Officer, India\n\n![](./img/avijit.jpeg)\n\n#### Avijit Dutta\n\nProduct Advisor, India\n\nA problem-solver, passionate storyteller, and product designer with broad repertoire\nacquired through working with 15+ brands in the last 19 Years across the world.\nAdvisor, mentor to a number of early-stage startups in India.\n\n#### Avijit Dutta\n\nProduct Advisor, India\n\n![](./img/sekhar nadella_1.jpeg)\n\n#### Sekhar Nadella\n\nTechnical Advisor, USA\n\nTech strategist with over 30 years of experience in developing payment solutions and\nfostering engineering excellence for scalable growth. Passionate about\nentrepreneurship, community engagement, and mentoring youth.\n\n#### Sekhar Nadella\n\nTechnical Advisor, USA\n\n![](./img/archana.jpeg)\n\n#### Archana Praveen\n\nDesign and Marketing, India\n\n#### Archana Praveen\n\nDesign and Marketing, India\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n###### DOWNLOAD APP ON\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n- [Privacy Policy](privacy-policy.html)\n- [Terms of Use](term-of-use.html)\n- [Terms for Organizations](terms-of-use-for-organizations.html)\n- [Parental Consent](parent-control.html)\n\n- [Funds Organizers](funds-organizers.html)\n- [Funds Donors](funds-donor.html)\n- [Ticket Policy](ticket-policy.html)\n\n- Chesterfield, Missouri, USA 63005\n- [![](./img/facebook.svg)](https://www.facebook.com/featsclub)[![](./img/instagram.svg)](https://www.instagram.com/featsclub)[![](./img/linkedin.svg)](https://www.linkedin.com/company/featsclub)\n\n- [![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub)\n\n\nDemo inquiries: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)\n\nPayments, refunds, and other queries:Please contact your organizers.\n\n©\nFeatsClub.       "markdown": "### **Introduction**\n\nFeatsClub is a platform that enables organizations (\"Organizers\") to create and manage\nticketed events for their users. We facilitate the ticketing process, collect a nominal\nprocessing fee, and provide tools to support both Organizers and Ticket Holders. By using\nFeatsClub, both Organizers and Ticket Holders agree to comply with this Ticket Policy, our\nTerms of Service, Privacy Policy, and all applicable legal and tax requirements.\n\nThis Ticket Policy is governed by the laws of the State of Missouri, and any disputes will be\nresolved exclusively in the courts located in Chesterfield, Missouri, in accordance with the\ngoverning law clause in FeatsClub's Terms of Service.\n\n### **Children Under the Age of 13**\n\n###### Facilitation Only\n\nFeatsClub acts solely as an intermediary, providing tools for Organizers to manage events\nand for Ticket Holders to purchase tickets. FeatsClub is not the producer or organizer\nof any events and is not responsible for event content, quality, or fulfillment.\n\n###### Processing Fee\n\nFeatsClub charges a non-refundable processing fee for tickets sold through the platform.\nThis fee is disclosed during the checkout process and may be paid by the Ticket Holder\nor absorbed by the Organizer, as determined by the Organizer.\n\n###### Optional Tax Tools\n\nFeatsClub provides optional tools for tax calculation based on event location. These\ntools are intended to assist Organizers but do not guarantee compliance with tax\nregulations. Organizers are advised to consult with tax professionals.\n\n###### Liability Limitation\n\nFeatsClub is not responsible for the accuracy of tax calculations or any event-related\nliabilities, including personal injury, property damage, or other losses incurred as a\nresult of event attendance.\n\n### **Organizer Responsibilities**\n\n###### Event Management\n\nOrganizers are responsible for planning, promoting, and executing their events, including\nproviding accurate and detailed event information to Ticket Holders.\n\n###### Tax Compliance\n\n- **Sales Tax Determination:** Organizers must determine if their event is subject\nto state and local sales taxes. FeatsClub may assist with tax calculations, but\ncompliance is the Organizer’s responsibility.\n- **Threshold Monitoring:** Organizers must monitor their activity to determine if\nstate-specific economic nexus thresholds are met (e.g., $100,000 in sales or 200\ntransactions within a calendar year) and take appropriate action.\n\n- **Tax Collection and Remittance:** Organizers are responsible for collecting and\nremitting applicable sales taxes to relevant authorities if they meet physical or\neconomic nexus criteria.\n\n- **State Registration:** Organizers meeting state thresholds must register with\nappropriate tax authorities to obtain a sales tax permit.\n\n- **Tax-Exempt Events:** Organizers hosting tax-exempt events must comply with\nstate-specific exemption requirements and provide documentation during event setup.\n\n- **Transparency:** Organizers must clearly indicate whether ticket prices include\nsales tax or if it will be added during checkout.\n\n\n###### Refund and Cancellation Policies\n\nOrganizers must establish, communicate, and display their refund and cancellation\npolicies during the ticket purchase process. FeatsClub is not responsible for processing\nrefunds or handling cancellations unless otherwise agreed.\n\n###### Record-Keeping and Reporting\n\nOrganizers must maintain accurate records of ticket sales, taxes collected, and any\nexemptions claimed for at least seven years or as required by law. FeatsClub provides\nsales reports to assist with filing obligations.\n\n###### Insurance Recommendation\n\nOrganizers are encouraged to obtain event liability insurance to cover risks not assumed\nby FeatsClub.\n\n### **Ticket Holder Responsibilities**\n\n###### Review of Event Details\n\nTicket Holders are responsible for reviewing event details before purchasing tickets,\nincluding date, time, location, and any restrictions or requirements set by the\nOrganizer.\n\n###### Adherence to Organizer Policies\n\nBy purchasing a ticket, Ticket Holders agree to abide by the Organizer’s policies,\nincluding refund, cancellation, and event conduct rules.\n\n###### Contacting Organizers\n\nFor questions or issues related to an event, including refunds or cancellations, Ticket\nHolders must contact the Organizer directly.\n\n###### Waiver of Rights\n\nTicket Holders waive any recourse against FeatsClub for event-related issues unless\notherwise specified by applicable law.\n\n### **Dispute Resolution**\n\n###### Organizer-Ticket Holder Disputes\n\nFeatsClub does not mediate disputes between Organizers and Ticket Holders, including\nthose related to refunds, cancellations, or taxes. Such disputes must be resolved\ndirectly between the parties.\n\n###### Arbitration Clause\n\nAny disputes related to the use of FeatsClub’s platform will be resolved through binding\narbitration under the rules of the Arbitration Organization specified in FeatsClub’s\nTerms of Service.\n\n###### Tax Disputes\n\nDisputes related to taxes (e.g., incorrect rates or remittance) are the sole\nresponsibility of the Organizer and must be addressed with the relevant tax authority.\n\n\n### **Limitation of Liability**\n\n###### Platform Provision\n\nFeatsClub provides the platform \"as is\" and disclaims all warranties, express or implied,\nrelated to its services.\n\n###### Force Majeure\n\nFeatsClub is not liable for any delays or failures in performance due to causes beyond\nits control, including natural disasters, pandemics, or other unforeseeable events.\n\n###### Event Liability\n\nFeatsClub is not liable for any personal injury, property damage, or other losses\nincurred as a result of event attendance. Claims related to events must be directed to\nthe Organizer.\n\n### **Amendments to This Policy**\n\nFeatsClub reserves the right to modify this Ticket Policy at any time. Changes will be\ncommunicated through official channels and will take effect 30 days after posting.\n\nContinued use of FeatsClub constitutes acceptance of the revised terms. Previous versions of\nthis policy will be archived for reference in case of disputes.\n\n### **Contact Us**\n\nFor any questions or further assistance regarding this policy, please contact FeatsClub\nsupport at:\n\nFeatsClub Inc.\n\n16948 BottleBrush Ct, Chesterfield, Missouri, 63005\n\nEmail: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)",

        "title": "Ticket Policy - FeatsClub",

      "markdown": "[![](./img/logo-black.svg)](index.html)\n\n[Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n- [Contact Us](contact-us.html)\n- [Sign In](https://my.featsclub.com)\n\n![](./img/support-hero.png)\n\n# Elevate Your Experience with Feats AI Your Personalized Knowledge Assistant\n\nEffortlessly navigate FeatsClub with Feats AI. Supporting individuals and\norganizations in extracurricular and community service needs.\n\n## Product Support and Account Deletion\n\n##### Who is the developer or author of FeatsClub app?\n\nFeatsClub is developed by a Delaware registered corporation. For details about the team driving the\nFeatsClub platform, visit their About page. You can contact the team for product questions or\nqueries at [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com) , and for product\nsupport, data misuse reports, bug reports, or other issues at [appsupport@featsclub.com](mailto:appsupport@featsclub.com) .\n\n\n##### How to reach FeatsClub for product support and questions?\n\nFeatsClub provides a product assistant called Feats AI on the homepage where users can click to ask\nany product-specific questions. Additionally, for direct contact with developers regarding product\nsupport or to report issues, users are encouraged to email [appsupport@featsclub.com](mailto:appsupport@featsclub.com). Please include the email\nused to set up your account, your question, and any additional details such as screenshots or\ndocuments that can assist FeatsClub in promptly resolving the issue.\n\n##### How do I deactivate or delete FeatsClub account?\n\nFeatsClub only offers an option to delete your account, not temporary deactivation. To delete your\naccount, go to Settings in FeatsClub app drawer menu and then Account Management. There, you will\nfind an option to delete your account.\n\n\n##### What types of data are deleted or kept and for how long when a user  deletes their account?\n\nWhen a user deletes their account, FeatsClub deletes all their data permanently, and it cannot be\nrecovered. Users will receive an explicit warning to confirm the deletion before proceeding. After\ndeletion, only the unique user ID associated with the account and the deletion time are stored for\nrecord-keeping and handling any queries.\n\n\n##### What should I do if I forget my password?\n\nTo reset your password for the FeatsClub app, you go to the login page and look for the \"Forgot\nPassword\" link. Click that link and follow the instructions to reset your password. If you have any\ntrouble, feel free to contact FeatsClub support at [appsupport@featsclub.com](mailto:appsupport@featsclub.com) from your FeatsClub\nregistered email.\n\n##### Does FeatsClub offer support in multiple languages?\n\nRight now, the only language that is supported for the FeatsClub platform is English. However, stay\ntuned for further updates as this can change in the future!\n\n![](./img/footer-shape.png)![](./img/footerWrappertab.png)![](./img/logo-black.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n![](./img/logo-white.svg)\n\n###### DOWNLOAD APP ON\n\n[![](./img/google-play.svg)](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.featsclub&pli=1)[![](./img/app-store.svg)](https://apps.apple.com/us/app/featsclub/id6473748399)\n\n- [Privacy Policy](privacy-policy.html)\n- [Terms of Use](term-of-use.html)\n- [Terms for Organizations](terms-of-use-for-organizations.html)\n- [Parental Consent](parent-control.html)\n\n- [Funds Organizers](funds-organizers.html)\n- [Funds Donors](funds-donor.html)\n- [Ticket Policy](ticket-policy.html)\n\n- Chesterfield, Missouri, USA 63005\n- [![](./img/facebook.svg)](https://www.facebook.com/featsclub)[![](./img/instagram.svg)](https://www.instagram.com/featsclub)[![](./img/linkedin.svg)](https://www.linkedin.com/company/featsclub)\n\n\nDemo inquiries: [contact@featsclub.com](mailto:contact@featsclub.com)\n\nPayments, refunds, and other queries:Please contact your organizers.",

    {
      "markdown": "As a platform that enables fundraising by registered organizations, FeatsClub requires\norganizers to review and adhere to the following terms before launching a fundraising\ncampaign. These guidelines ensure transparency, compliance, and responsible use of\nFeatsClub’s platform.\n\n\n## Organizer Verification and Compliance Requirements\n\n- **Verification:** All organizers must complete FeatsClub’s verification process,\n   which includes validating the organization’s identity, legal status, and mission.\n\n- **Compliance with Local Laws:** mOrganizers are solely responsible for ensuring that\n   all fundraising activities comply with relevant local, state, and national regulations\n   in their jurisdiction.\n\n- **Know Your Customer (KYC) Compliance:** FeatsClub reserves the right to perform\n   additional verification on organizations to meet anti-fraud and KYC standards, including\n   requesting legal documents and background information if necessary.\n\n\n## Processing Charges and Configurations\n\n- **Understanding Processing Charges:** Organizations acknowledge that processing\n   charges apply to all donations processed through FeatsClub. These charges include\n   FeatsClub’s operational costs and fees from third-party payment facilitators.\n   Organizations must review these charges prior to campaign launch.\n\n- **Absorbing or Passing on Fees:** Organizations can choose to either absorb these\n   processing charges themselves or configure the settings to have donors cover the\n   additional cost. FeatsClub provides the necessary settings for organizations to make\n   this choice transparently during the campaign setup process.\n\n- **On-Behalf Campaigns:** FeatsClub allows users to launch “on-behalf” campaigns with\n   the organization’s permission. However, both FeatsClub and the organization reserve the\n   right to terminate any on-behalf campaign that is found to be misaligned with the\n   organization’s mission or platform guidelines.\n\n\n## Campaign Responsibilities\n\n- **Campaign Purpose and Accuracy:** Organizers are required to provide a clear,\n   accurate description of their campaign’s purpose, goals, and intended use of funds.\n   Misrepresentation of campaign details is strictly prohibited.\n\n- **Use of Funds:** Funds raised through FeatsClub must be used exclusively for the\n   stated purpose of the campaign. Any misuse of funds may result in investigation,\n   sanctions, and potential termination of the organizer’s account.\n\n- **Transparency with Donors:** Organizers must maintain transparency with donors by\n   providing updates on the campaign’s progress and the allocation of funds, especially for\n   campaigns that reach significant milestones or change in scope.\n\n- **Nonprofit status and tax receipts:** Organizations that collect donations as a\n   nonprofit (501(c)(3) or equivalent) are solely responsible for maintaining their\n   tax-exempt status and for issuing valid tax receipts to donors. FeatsClub does not\n   verify tax-exempt status and does not issue tax receipts on behalf of organizations.\n\n\n## Consequences for Policy Violations\n\n- **Investigation Process:** FeatsClub will investigate any reports of policy\n   violations, including fund misuse, fraud, or misrepresentation. The organizer agrees to\n   cooperate fully with any requests for information during the investigation.\n\n- **Sanctions and Account Termination:** If an organizer is found to be in violation\n   of FeatsClub’s policies, FeatsClub reserves the right to impose sanctions, including\n   temporary suspension or permanent termination of the organization’s account. Violations\n   may also be reported to legal authorities if deemed necessary.\n\n\n## Dispute Resolution and Refund Process\n\n- **Donor Disputes:** In cases where donors raise concerns or disputes, FeatsClub\n   encourages organizers to address these directly with the donor. FeatsClub may facilitate\n   communication but is not responsible for handling refunds or managing dispute outcomes.\n\n- **Refund Policy:** All donations are final. Organizers are expected to honor their\n   commitments to donors as outlined in the campaign. If an organizer chooses to offer\n   refunds, it is solely at their discretion and not facilitated by FeatsClub.\n\n\n## Privacy and Data Security\n\n- **Confidentiality of Donor Information:** Organizers must respect donor privacy and\n   agree not to misuse or share donor information outside the scope of the campaign without\n   explicit consent.\n\n- **Data Protection Compliance:** Organizers are required to adhere to applicable data\n   protection laws, such as GDPR or CCPA, when handling donor information.\n\n\n## Adherence to FeatsClub Terms of Use for Organizations\n\n- **Organizational Terms of Use:** In addition to this Fundraising Policy, all\n   organizations must adhere to FeatsClub’s [Terms of Use for\\\n   Organizations](https://www.featsclub.com/organization_terms_conditions), agreed to at the time of registration. Violations of these Terms\n   of Use may result in account suspension or termination.\n\n\n\n## Modification of Terms\n\n- **Policy Updates:** FeatsClub reserves the right to modify this policy. Organizers\n   will be notified of any updates, and it is their responsibility to review and comply\n   with current terms for continued use of the platform.\n\n\n\n## Liability and Indemnification\n\n- **Indemnity:** By using FeatsClub’s platform, organizers agree to hold FeatsClub\n   harmless from any disputes, legal issues, or financial claims that may arise from their\n   fundraising activities.\n\n- **Waiver of Liability:** FeatsClub does not assume responsibility for the\n   fundraising activities of organizers and disclaims liability for the mismanagement or\n   misuse of funds by any organization on its platform.",
